




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

dīghanikāyau


mahāvaggapāḷi


1. mahāpadānasuttaṃ

pubbainivāsapaṭisaṃyuttakathā

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai karairikuṭikāyaṃ| atha khau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ karairimaṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ pubbainivāsapaṭisaṃyuttā dhammī kathā udapādi – ‘‘itipi pubbainivāsau, itipi pubbainivāsau’’ti|

2. assausi khau bhagavā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina karairimaṇḍalamāḷau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi, nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāyanuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā; kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ pacchābhattaṃ  piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ karairimaṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ  pubbainivāsapaṭisaṃyuttā dhammī kathā udapādi – ‘itipi pubbainivāsau itipi pubbainivāsau’ti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā| atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

3. ‘‘icchaiyyātha  nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, pubbainivāsapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ sautu’’nti? ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau; yaṃ bhagavā pubbainivāsapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karaiyya, bhagavatau sutvā bhagavatau vacanaṃ sutvā (syā॰) bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha,sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

4. ‘‘itau sau, bhikkhavai, aikanavutikappai yaṃ aikanavutau kappau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| itau sau, bhikkhavai, aikatiṃsai kappai aikatiṃ sakappau (sī॰) aikatiṃ sau kappau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yaṃ sikhī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| tasmiñ‍ñaiva khau, bhikkhavai, aikatiṃsai kappai vaissabhū bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| imasmiñ‍ñaiva imasmiṃ (katthacī) khau, bhikkhavai, bhaddakappai kakusandhau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| imasmiñ‍ñaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhaddakappai kauṇāgamanau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| imasmiñ‍ñaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhaddakappai kassapau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| imasmiñ‍ñaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhaddakappai ahaṃ aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai uppan‍nau|

5. ‘‘vipassī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau khattiyau jātiyā  ahausi, khattiyakulai udapādi| sikhī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau khattiyau jātiyā ahausi, khattiyakulai udapādi| vaissabhū, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau  khattiyau jātiyā ahausi, khattiyakulai udapādi| kakusandhau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau brāhmaṇau jātiyā ahausi, brāhmaṇakulai udapādi| kauṇāgamanau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau  brāhmaṇau jātiyā ahausi, brāhmaṇakulai udapādi| kassapau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau brāhmaṇau jātiyā ahausi, brāhmaṇakulai udapādi| ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau khattiyau jātiyā ahausiṃ, khattiyakulai uppan‍nau|

6. ‘‘vipassī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kauṇḍañ‍ñau gauttaina ahausi| sikhī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kauṇḍañ‍ñau gauttaina ahausi| vaissabhū, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kauṇḍañ‍ñau gauttaina ahausi| kakusandhau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kassapau gauttaina ahausi| kauṇāgamanau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kassapau gauttaina ahausi| kassapau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kassapau gauttaina ahausi| ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau gautamau gauttaina ahausiṃ|

7. ‘‘vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa asītivassasahassāni  āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| sikhissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa sattativassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| vaissabhussa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa saṭṭhivassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa cattālīsavassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tiṃsavassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ  ahausi| kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa vīsativassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi appakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ; yau ciraṃ jīvati, sau vassasataṃ appaṃ vā bhiyyau|

8. ‘‘vipassī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pāṭaliyā mūlai abhisambuddhau| sikhī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau puṇḍarīkassa mūlai abhisambuddhau| vaissabhū, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau sālassa mūlai abhisambuddhau| kakusandhau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau sirīsassa mūlai abhisambuddhau| kauṇāgamanau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau udumbarassa mūlai abhisambuddhau| kassapau, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau nigraudhassa mūlai abhisambuddhau| ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau assatthassa mūlai abhisambuddhau|

9. ‘‘vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa khaṇḍatissaṃ  nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| sikhissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa abhibhūsambhavaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| vaissabhussa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa sauṇuttaraṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa vidhurasañ‍jīvaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa bhiyyausuttaraṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ  bhaddayugaṃ| kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tissabhāradvājaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi sāriputtamauggal‍lānaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ|

10. ‘‘vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ| aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ, aikau  sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi bhikkhusatasahassaṃ, aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi asītibhikkhusahassāni| vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa imai tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ|

‘‘sikhissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ| aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi bhikkhusatasahassaṃ, aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi asītibhikkhusahassāni, aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi sattatibhikkhusahassāni| sikhissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa imai tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ|

‘‘vaissabhussa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ| aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi asītibhikkhusahassāni, aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi sattatibhikkhusahassāni, aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi  saṭṭhibhikkhusahassāni| vaissabhussa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa imai tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ|

‘‘kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi  cattālīsabhikkhusahassāni| kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa ayaṃ aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ|

‘‘kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau  sammāsambuddhassa aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi tiṃsabhikkhusahassāni| kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa ayaṃ aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ|

‘‘kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi vīsatibhikkhusahassāni| kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa ayaṃ aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ|

‘‘mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi aikau  sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni| mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ|

11. ‘‘vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa asaukau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| sikhissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa khaimaṅkarau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| vaissabhussa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa upasantau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa buddhijau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa sautthijau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa sabbamittau nāma  bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi ānandau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau|

12. ‘‘vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa bandhumā nāma rājā pitā ahausi|  bandhumatī nāma daivī mātā ahausi janaitti janaittī (syā॰)| bandhumassa rañ‍ñau bandhumatī nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi|

‘‘sikhissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aruṇau  nāma rājā pitā ahausi| pabhāvatī nāma daivī mātā ahausi janaitti| aruṇassa rañ‍ñau aruṇavatī nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi|

‘‘vaissabhussa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa suppatitau nāma suppatītau nāma (syā॰) rājā pitā ahausi| vassavatī nāma yasavatī nāma (syā॰ pī॰) daivī mātā ahausi janaitti| suppatitassa rañ‍ñau anaumaṃ nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi|

‘‘kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aggidattau nāma brāhmaṇau pitā ahausi| visākhā nāma brāhmaṇī mātā ahausi janaitti| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina khaimau nāma rājā ahausi| khaimassa rañ‍ñau khaimavatī nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi|

‘‘kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa yañ‍ñadattau nāma brāhmaṇau pitā ahausi| uttarā nāma brāhmaṇī mātā ahausi janaitti| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina saubhau nāma rājā ahausi| saubhassa rañ‍ñau saubhavatī nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi|

‘‘kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa brahmadattau nāma brāhmaṇau pitā ahausi| dhanavatī nāma brāhmaṇī mātā ahausi janaitti| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina kikī  nāma kiṃ kī nāma (syā॰) rājā ahausi| kikissa rañ‍ñau bārāṇasī nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi|

‘‘mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitarahi suddhaudanau nāma rājā pitā ahausi| māyā nāma daivī mātā ahausi janaitti| kapilavatthu nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna  sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

13. atha  khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā| yatra hi nāma tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai  jātitaupi anussarissati, nāmataupi anussarissati, gauttataupi anussarissati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarissati, sāvakayugataupi anussarissati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi anussarissati – ‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, tathāgatassaiva nu khau aisā dhammadhātu suppaṭividdhā, yassā dhammadhātuyā suppaṭividdhattā tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jātitaupi anussarati, nāmataupi anussarati, gauttataupi anussarati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarati, sāvakayugataupi anussarati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi anussarati – ‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā tai  bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’ti, udāhu daivatā tathāgatassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ, yaina tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai  pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jātitaupi anussarati, nāmataupi anussarati, gauttataupi anussarati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarati, sāvakayugataupi anussarati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi anussarati – ‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā  tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’’’ti| ayañ‍ca hidaṃ taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā vippakatā hauti|

14. atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina karairimaṇḍalamāḷau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāyanuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā; kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau ayaṃ antarākathā udapādi – ‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāma tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jātitaupi anussarissati, nāmataupi anussarissati, gauttataupi anussarissati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarissati, sāvakayugataupi anussarissati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi  anussarissati – ‘‘aivaṃjac‍cā  tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’’ti| kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, tathāgatassaiva nu khau aisā dhammadhātu suppaṭividdhā, yassā dhammadhātuyā suppaṭividdhattā tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jātitaupi anussarati, nāmataupi anussarati, gauttataupi anussarati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarati, sāvakayugataupi anussarati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi anussarati – ‘‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’’ti| udāhu daivatā tathāgatassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ, yaina tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jātitaupi anussarati, nāmataupi anussarati, gauttataupi anussarati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarati, sāvakayugataupi anussarati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi anussarati – ‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā  tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’ti? ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

15. ‘‘tathāgatassaivaisā, bhikkhavai, dhammadhātu suppaṭividdhā, yassā dhammadhātuyā suppaṭividdhattā tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jātitaupi anussarati, nāmataupi anussarati, gauttataupi anussarati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarati, sāvakayugataupi anussarati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi anussarati – ‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’ti| daivatāpi tathāgatassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ, yaina tathāgatau atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivattai jātitaupi anussarati, nāmataupi anussarati, gauttataupi anussarati, āyuppamāṇataupi anussarati, sāvakayugataupi  anussarati, sāvakasan‍nipātataupi anussarati – ‘aivaṃjac‍cā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttā aivaṃsīlā aivaṃdhammā aivaṃpañ‍ñā aivaṃvihārī aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’ti|

‘‘icchaiyyātha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhiyyausaumattāya pubbainivāsapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ  dhammiṃ kathaṃ sautu’’nti? ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau; yaṃ bhagavā bhiyyausaumattāya pubbainivāsapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karaiyya, bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

16. ‘‘itau sau, bhikkhavai, aikanavutikappai yaṃ vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| vipassī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau khattiyau jātiyā ahausi, khattiyakulai udapādi| vipassī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kauṇḍañ‍ñau gauttaina ahausi| vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa asītivassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| vipassī, bhikkhavai, bhagavā arahaṃ  sammāsambuddhau pāṭaliyā mūlai abhisambuddhau| vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa khaṇḍatissaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ| aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ, aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi bhikkhusatasahassaṃ, aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi asītibhikkhusahassāni| vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa imai tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ| vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa asaukau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa bandhumā nāma rājā pitā ahausi| bandhumatī nāma  daivī mātā ahausi janaitti| bandhumassa rañ‍ñau bandhumatī nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi|

baudhisattadhammatā

17. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī baudhisattau tusitā kāyā cavitvā satau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kami| ayamaittha dhammatā|

18. ‘‘dhammatā, aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau tusitā kāyā cavitvā mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati| atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā, yattha pimai candimasūriyā aivaṃmahiddhikā aivaṃmahānubhāvā ābhāya nānubhaunti, tatthapi appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā, taipi tainaubhāsaina  añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti – ‘añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nā’ti| ayañ‍ca dasasahassī laukadhātu saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| appamāṇau ca uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| ayamaittha dhammatā|

19. ‘‘dhammatā  aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, cattārau naṃ daivaputtā catuddisaṃ cātuddisaṃ (syā॰) rakkhāya upagacchanti – ‘mā naṃ baudhisattaṃ vā baudhisattamātaraṃ vā manussau vā amanussau vā kauci vā vihaiṭhaisī’ti| ayamaittha dhammatā|

20. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, pakatiyā sīlavatī baudhisattamātā hauti, viratā pāṇātipātā, viratā adin‍nādānā, viratā kāmaisumicchācārā, viratā musāvādā, viratā surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā| ayamaittha dhammatā|

21. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, na baudhisattamātu purisaisu mānasaṃ uppaj‍jati kāmaguṇūpasaṃhitaṃ, anatik‍kamanīyā ca baudhisattamātā hauti kainaci purisaina rattacittaina| ayamaittha dhammatā|

22. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau  hauti, lābhinī baudhisattamātā hauti pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ| sā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricāraiti| ayamaittha dhammatā|

23. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, na baudhisattamātu kaucidaiva ābādhau uppaj‍jati| sukhinī baudhisattamātā hauti akilantakāyā, baudhisattañ‍ca baudhisattamātā tiraukucchigataṃ passati sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau sabbākārasampan‍nau| tatrāssa tatrassa (syā॰) suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau hatthai karitvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya – ‘ayaṃ khau maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau sabbākārasampan‍nau| tatridaṃ suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau  mātukucchiṃ auk‍kantau hauti, na baudhisattamātu kaucidaiva ābādhau uppaj‍jati, sukhinī baudhisattamātā hauti akilantakāyā, baudhisattañ‍ca  baudhisattamātā tiraukucchigataṃ passati sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriyaṃ| ayamaittha dhammatā|

24. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, sattāhajātai baudhisattai baudhisattamātā kālaṅkarauti tusitaṃ kāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| ayamaittha dhammatā|

25. ‘‘dhammatā  aisā, bhikkhavai, yathā añ‍ñā itthikā nava vā dasa vā māsai gabbhaṃ kucchinā pariharitvā vijāyanti, na haivaṃ baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā vijāyati| dasaiva māsāni baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā kucchinā pariharitvā vijāyati| ayamaittha dhammatā|

26. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yathā añ‍ñā itthikā nisin‍nā vā nipan‍nā vā vijāyanti, na haivaṃ baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā vijāyati| ṭhitāva baudhisattaṃ baudhisattamātā vijāyati| ayamaittha dhammatā|

27. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, daivā paṭhamaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti, pacchā manussā| ayamaittha dhammatā|

28. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, appattauva baudhisattau pathaviṃ hauti, cattārau naṃ daivaputtā paṭiggahaitvā mātu puratau ṭhapainti – ‘attamanā, daivi, hauhi; mahaisakkhau tai puttau uppan‍nau’ti| ayamaittha dhammatā|

29. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, visadauva nikkhamati amakkhitau udaina uddaina (syā॰), udaraina (katthaci) amakkhitau saimhaina amakkhitau ruhiraina amakkhitau kainaci asucinā suddhau visuddhau (syā॰) visadau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, maṇiratanaṃ kāsikai vatthai nikkhittaṃ naiva maṇiratanaṃ kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ makkhaiti, nāpi kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ maṇiratanaṃ makkhaiti| taṃ kissa  haitu? ubhin‍naṃ suddhattā| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, visadauva nikkhamati amakkhitau, udaina amakkhitau  saimhaina  amakkhitau ruhiraina amakkhitau kainaci asucinā suddhau visadau| ayamaittha dhammatā|

30. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, dvai udakassa  dhārā antalikkhā pātubhavanti – aikā sītassa aikā uṇhassa yaina baudhisattassa udakakic‍caṃ karaunti mātu ca| ayamaittha dhammatā|

31. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, sampatijātau baudhisattau samaihi pādaihi patiṭṭhahitvā uttarābhimukhau uttarainābhimukhau (syā॰) uttarainamukhau (ka॰) sattapadavītihāraina gacchati saitamhi chattai anudhāriyamānai, sabbā ca disā anuvilaukaiti, āsabhiṃ vācaṃ bhāsati ‘aggauhamasmi laukassa, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, saiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, ayamantimā jāti, natthidāni punabbhavau’ti| ayamaittha dhammatā|

32. ‘‘dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau  pātubhavati, atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā, yattha pimai candimasūriyā aivaṃmahiddhikā aivaṃmahānubhāvā ābhāya nānubhaunti, tatthapi appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā, taipi tainaubhāsaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti – ‘añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nā’ti| ayañ‍ca dasasahassī laukadhātu saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati appamāṇau ca uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| ayamaittha dhammatā|

dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇā

33. ‘‘jātai  khau pana, bhikkhavai, vipassimhi kumārai bandhumatau rañ‍ñau paṭivaidaisuṃ – ‘puttau tai, daiva daiva tai (ka॰), jātau, taṃ daivau passatū’ti| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā vipassiṃ kumāraṃ, disvā naimittai brāhmaṇai  āmantāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘passantu bhauntau naimittā brāhmaṇā kumāra’nti| addasaṃsu khau, bhikkhavai, naimittā brāhmaṇā vipassiṃ kumāraṃ, disvā bandhumantaṃ rājānaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘attamanau, daiva, hauhi, mahaisakkhau tai puttau uppan‍nau, lābhā tai, mahārāja, suladdhaṃ tai, mahārāja, yassa tai kulai aivarūpau puttau uppan‍nau| ayañhi, daiva, kumārau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni sattaratanāni bhavanti| saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pariṇāyakaratanamaiva  sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭacchadau|

34. ‘katamaihi cāyaṃ, daiva, kumārau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni sattaratanāni bhavanti| saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭacchadau|

35. ‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau suppatiṭṭhitapādau| yaṃ pāyaṃ, daiva, kumārau suppatiṭṭhitapādau| idampissa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘imassa, daiva imassa hi daiva (?), kumārassa haiṭṭhā pādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātāni sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbākāraparipūrāni| yampi, imassa  daiva, kumārassa haiṭṭhā pādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātāni sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbākāraparipūrāni, idampissa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘ayañhi  daiva, kumārau āyatapaṇhī…pai॰…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau dīghaṅgulī…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau mudutalunahatthapādau…

‘ayañhi, daiva kumārau jālahatthapādau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau ussaṅkhapādau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau aiṇijaṅghau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau ṭhitakauva anaunamantau ubhauhi pāṇitalaihi jaṇṇukāni parimasati parāmasati (ka॰) parimaj‍jati…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau kausauhitavatthaguyhau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau suvaṇṇavaṇṇau kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau sukhumacchavī; sukhumattā  chaviyā rajaujal‍laṃ kāyai na upalimpati upalippati (syā॰) …

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau aikaikalaumau; aikaikāni laumāni laumakūpaisu jātāni…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau uddhaggalaumau; uddhaggāni laumāni jātāni nīlāni añ‍janavaṇṇāni kuṇḍalāvaṭṭāni dakkhiṇāvaṭṭakajātāni…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau brahmujugattau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau sattussadau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau sīhapubbaddhakāyau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau citantaraṃsau pitantaraṃsau (syā॰) …

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau yāvatak‍vassa kāyau tāvatak‍vassa byāmau, yāvatak‍vassa byāmau, tāvatak‍vassa kāyau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau samavaṭṭakkhandhau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau rasaggasaggī…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau sīhahanu…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau cattālīsadantau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau samadantau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau aviraḷadantau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau susuk‍kadāṭhau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau pahūtajivhau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau brahmassarau karavīkabhāṇī…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau abhinīlanaittau…

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau gaupakhumau…

imassa, daiva, kumārassa uṇṇā bhamukantarai jātā audātā mudutūlasan‍nibhā| yampi imassa daiva kumārassa uṇṇā bhamukantarai jātā audātā mudutūlasan‍nibhā, idampimassa  mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘ayañhi, daiva, kumārau uṇhīsasīsau| yaṃ pāyaṃ, daiva, kumārau uṇhīsasīsau, idampissa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

36. ‘imaihi khau ayaṃ, daiva, kumārau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni sattaratanāni bhavanti| saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina dhammaina samaina (syā॰) abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai khau pana  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭacchadau’ti|

vipassīsamañ‍ñā

37. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā naimittai brāhmaṇai ahataihi vatthaihi acchādāpaitvā acchādaitvā (syā॰) sabbakāmaihi santappaisi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā vipassissa kumārassa dhātiyau upaṭṭhāpaisi| añ‍ñā khīraṃ pāyainti, añ‍ñā nhāpainti, añ‍ñā dhārainti, añ‍ñā aṅkaina pariharanti| jātassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, vipassissa kumārassa saitacchattaṃ dhārayittha divā caiva rattiñ‍ca – ‘mā naṃ sītaṃ vā  uṇhaṃ vā tiṇaṃ vā rajau vā ussāvau vā bādhayitthā’ti| jātau khau pana, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bahunau janassa piyau ahausi manāpau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uppalaṃ vā  padumaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ vā bahunau janassa piyaṃ manāpaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bahunau janassa piyau ahausi manāpau| svāssudaṃ aṅkainaiva aṅkaṃ parihariyati|

38. ‘‘jātau khau pana, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau mañ‍jussarau ca kumārau brahmassarau mañ‍jussarau ca (sī॰ ka॰) ahausi vaggussarau ca madhurassarau ca paimaniyassarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, himavantai pabbatai karavīkā nāma sakuṇajāti mañ‍jussarā ca vaggussarā ca madhurassarā ca paimaniyassarā ca; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau mañ‍jussarau ca ahausi vaggussarau ca madhurassarau ca paimaniyassarau ca|

39. ‘‘jātassa  khau pana, bhikkhavai, vipassissa kumārassa kammavipākajaṃ dibbacakkhu pāturahausi yaina sudaṃ yaina dūraṃ (syā॰) samantā yaujanaṃ passati divā caiva rattiñ‍ca|

40. ‘‘jātau khau pana, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau animisantau paikkhati saiyyathāpi daivā tāvatiṃsā| ‘animisantau kumārau paikkhatī’ti khau, bhikkhavai animisantau paikkhati, jātassa khau pana bhikkhavai (ka॰), vipassissa kumārassa ‘vipassī vipassī’ tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi|

41. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā atthakaraṇai aṭṭa karaṇai (syā॰) nisin‍nau vipassiṃ kumāraṃ aṅkai nisīdāpaitvā atthai anusāsati| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau pituaṅkai nisin‍nau vicaiyya  vicaiyya atthai panāyati ñāyaina aṭṭai panāyati ñāṇaina (syā॰)| vicaiyya vicaiyya kumārau atthai panāyati ñāyaināti khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa kumārassa bhiyyausaumattāya ‘vipassī vipassī’ tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi|

42. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā vipassissa kumārassa tayau pāsādai kārāpaisi, aikaṃ vassikaṃ aikaṃ haimantikaṃ aikaṃ gimhikaṃ; pañ‍ca kāmaguṇāni upaṭṭhāpaisi| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau vassikai pāsādai cattārau māsai vassikai pāsādai vassikai nippurisaihi tūriyaihi paricārayamānau na haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ aurauhatī’’ti|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

jiṇṇapurisau

43. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bahūnaṃ vassānaṃ bahūnaṃ vassasatānaṃ bahūnaṃ vassasahassānaṃ ac‍cayaina sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘yaujaihi, samma sārathi, bhaddāni bhaddāni yānāni uyyānabhūmiṃ gacchāma subhūmidassanāyā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā bhaddāni bhaddāni yānāni yaujaitvā vipassissa kumārassa paṭivaidaisi – ‘yuttāni khau tai, daiva, bhaddāni bhaddāni yānāni, yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bhaddaṃ bhaddaṃ yānaṃ bhadraṃ yānaṃ (syā॰), bhaddaṃ yānaṃ (pī॰) cattārau māsai (sī॰ pī॰) abhiruhitvā bhaddaihi bhaddaihi yānaihi uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyāsi|

44. ‘‘addasā khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ  niyyantau purisaṃ jiṇṇaṃ gaupānasivaṅkaṃ bhauggaṃ bhaggaṃ (syā॰) daṇḍaparāyanaṃ pavaidhamānaṃ gacchantaṃ āturaṃ  gatayaubbanaṃ| disvā sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘ayaṃ pana, samma sārathi, purisau kiṃkatau? kaisāpissa na yathā añ‍ñaisaṃ, kāyaupissa  na yathā añ‍ñaisa’nti| ‘aisau khau, daiva, jiṇṇau nāmā’ti| ‘kiṃ panaisau, samma sārathi, jiṇṇau nāmā’ti? ‘aisau khau, daiva, jiṇṇau nāma| na dāni taina ciraṃ jīvitabbaṃ bhavissatī’ti| ‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, ahampi jarādhammau, jaraṃ anatītau’ti? ‘tvañ‍ca, daiva, mayañ‍camha sabbai jarādhammā, jaraṃ anatītā’ti| ‘taina hi, samma sārathi, alaṃ dānaj‍ja uyyānabhūmiyā| itauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā tatauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāsi| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau antaipuraṃ gatau dukkhī dummanau pajjhāyati – ‘dhiratthu kira, bhau, jāti nāma, yatra hi nāma jātassa jarā pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti!

45. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā sārathiṃ āmantāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘kac‍ci, samma sārathi, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā abhiramittha? kac‍ci, samma sārathi, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā attamanau ahausī’ti? ‘na khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā abhiramittha, na khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā attamanau ahausī’ti| ‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, addasa kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau’ti? ‘addasā  khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau purisaṃ jiṇṇaṃ gaupānasivaṅkaṃ bhauggaṃ daṇḍaparāyanaṃ pavaidhamānaṃ gacchantaṃ āturaṃ gatayaubbanaṃ| disvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ pana, samma sārathi, purisau kiṃkatau, kaisāpissa na yathā añ‍ñaisaṃ, kāyaupissa  na yathā añ‍ñaisa’’nti? ‘‘aisau khau, daiva, jiṇṇau nāmā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panaisau, samma sārathi, jiṇṇau nāmā’’ti? ‘‘aisau khau, daiva, jiṇṇau nāma na dāni taina ciraṃ jīvitabbaṃ bhavissatī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, ahampi jarādhammau, jaraṃ anatītau’’ti? ‘‘tvañ‍ca, daiva, mayañ‍camha sabbai jarādhammā, jaraṃ anatītā’’ti|

‘‘‘taina hi, samma sārathi, alaṃ dānaj‍ja uyyānabhūmiyā, itauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāhī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau ahaṃ, daiva, vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā tatauva  antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāsiṃ| sau khau, daiva, kumārau antaipuraṃ gatau dukkhī dummanau pajjhāyati – ‘‘dhiratthu kira bhau jāti nāma, yatra hi nāma jātassa jarā pañ‍ñāyissatī’’’ti|

byādhitapurisau

46. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumassa rañ‍ñau aitadahausi –

‘mā haiva khau vipassī kumārau na raj‍jaṃ kāraisi, mā haiva vipassī kumārau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji, mā haiva naimittānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ assa vacana’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā vipassissa kumārassa bhiyyausaumattāya pañ‍ca kāmaguṇāni upaṭṭhāpaisi – ‘yathā vipassī kumārau raj‍jaṃ karaiyya, yathā vipassī kumārau na agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya, yathā naimittānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ micchā assa vacana’nti|

‘‘tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bahūnaṃ vassānaṃ…pai॰…

47. ‘‘addasā  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau purisaṃ ābādhikaṃ  dukkhitaṃ bāḷhagilānaṃ sakai muttakarīsai palipan‍naṃ saimānaṃ sayamānaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) añ‍ñaihi vuṭṭhāpiyamānaṃ añ‍ñaihi saṃvaisiyamānaṃ| disvā sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘ayaṃ pana, samma sārathi, purisau kiṃkatau? akkhīnipissa na yathā añ‍ñaisaṃ, saraupissa siraupissa (syā॰) na yathā añ‍ñaisa’nti? ‘aisau khau, daiva, byādhitau nāmā’ti| ‘kiṃ panaisau, samma sārathi, byādhitau nāmā’ti? ‘aisau khau, daiva, byādhitau nāma appaiva nāma tamhā ābādhā vuṭṭhahaiyyā’ti| ‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, ahampi byādhidhammau, byādhiṃ anatītau’ti? ‘tvañ‍ca, daiva, mayañ‍camha sabbai byādhidhammā, byādhiṃ anatītā’ti| ‘taina hi, samma sārathi, alaṃ dānaj‍ja uyyānabhūmiyā, itauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā tatauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāsi| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau antaipuraṃ gatau dukkhī dummanau pajjhāyati – ‘dhiratthu kira bhau jāti nāma, yatra hi nāma jātassa jarā pañ‍ñāyissati, byādhi pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti|

48. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā sārathiṃ āmantāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘kac‍ci, samma sārathi, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā abhiramittha, kac‍ci, samma  sārathi, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā attamanau ahausī’ti? ‘na khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā abhiramittha, na khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā attamanau ahausī’ti| ‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, addasa kumārau  uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau’ti? ‘addasā khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau purisaṃ ābādhikaṃ dukkhitaṃ bāḷhagilānaṃ sakai muttakarīsai  palipan‍naṃ saimānaṃ añ‍ñaihi vuṭṭhāpiyamānaṃ añ‍ñaihi saṃvaisiyamānaṃ| disvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ pana, samma sārathi, purisau kiṃkatau, akkhīnipissa na yathā añ‍ñaisaṃ, saraupissa na yathā añ‍ñaisa’’nti? ‘‘aisau khau, daiva, byādhitau nāmā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panaisau, samma sārathi, byādhitau nāmā’’ti? ‘‘aisau khau, daiva, byādhitau nāma appaiva nāma tamhā ābādhā vuṭṭhahaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, ahampi byādhidhammau, byādhiṃ anatītau’’ti? ‘‘tvañ‍ca, daiva, mayañ‍camha sabbai byādhidhammā, byādhiṃ anatītā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samma sārathi, alaṃ dānaj‍ja uyyānabhūmiyā, itauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau ahaṃ, daiva, vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā tatauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāsiṃ| sau khau, daiva, kumārau antaipuraṃ gatau dukkhī dummanau pajjhāyati – ‘‘‘dhiratthu kira bhau jāti nāma, yatra hi nāma jātassa jarā pañ‍ñāyissati, byādhi pañ‍ñāyissatī’’’ti|

kālaṅkatapurisau

49. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumassa rañ‍ñau aitadahausi – ‘mā haiva khau vipassī kumārau na raj‍jaṃ kāraisi, mā haiva vipassī kumārau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji, mā haiva naimittānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ assa vacana’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā vipassissa kumārassa bhiyyausaumattāya pañ‍ca kāmaguṇāni upaṭṭhāpaisi – ‘yathā vipassī  kumārau raj‍jaṃ karaiyya, yathā vipassī kumārau na agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya, yathā naimittānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ micchā assa vacana’nti|

‘‘tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bahūnaṃ vassānaṃ…pai॰…

50. ‘‘addasā  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau mahājanakāyaṃ san‍nipatitaṃ  nānārattānañ‍ca dussānaṃ vilātaṃ kayiramānaṃ| disvā sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘kiṃ nu khau, sau, samma sārathi, mahājanakāyau san‍nipatitau nānārattānañ‍ca dussānaṃ vilātaṃ kayiratī’ti? ‘aisau  khau, daiva, kālaṅkatau nāmā’ti| ‘taina hi, samma sārathi, yaina sau kālaṅkatau taina rathaṃ paisaihī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā yaina sau kālaṅkatau taina rathaṃ paisaisi| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau paitaṃ kālaṅkataṃ, disvā sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘kiṃ panāyaṃ, samma sārathi, kālaṅkatau nāmā’ti? ‘aisau khau, daiva, kālaṅkatau nāma| na dāni taṃ dakkhanti mātā vā pitā vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitā, saupi na dakkhissati mātaraṃ vā pitaraṃ vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitai’ti| ‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, ahampi maraṇadhammau maraṇaṃ anatītau; mampi na dakkhanti daivau vā daivī vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitā; ahampi na dakkhissāmi daivaṃ vā daiviṃ vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitai’ti? ‘tvañ‍ca, daiva, mayañ‍camha sabbai maraṇadhammā maraṇaṃ anatītā; tampi na dakkhanti daivau vā daivī vā añ‍ñai  vā ñātisālauhitā; tvampi na dakkhissasi daivaṃ vā daiviṃ vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitai’ti| ‘taina hi, samma sārathi, alaṃ dānaj‍ja uyyānabhūmiyā, itauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā tatauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāsi| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau antaipuraṃ gatau dukkhī dummanau pajjhāyati – ‘dhiratthu kira, bhau, jāti nāma, yatra hi nāma jātassa jarā pañ‍ñāyissati, byādhi pañ‍ñāyissati, maraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti|

51. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā sārathiṃ āmantāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘kac‍ci, samma sārathi, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā abhiramittha, kac‍ci, samma sārathi, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā attamanau ahausī’ti? ‘na  khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā abhiramittha, na khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiyā attamanau ahausī’ti| ‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, addasa kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau’ti? ‘addasā khau, daiva, kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau mahājanakāyaṃ san‍nipatitaṃ nānārattānañ‍ca dussānaṃ vilātaṃ kayiramānaṃ| disvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, sau, samma sārathi, mahājanakāyau san‍nipatitau nānārattānañ‍ca dussānaṃ vilātaṃ kayiratī’’ti? ‘‘aisau khau, daiva, kālaṅkatau nāmā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samma sārathi, yaina sau kālaṅkatau taina rathaṃ paisaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ daivā’’ti khau ahaṃ, daiva, vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā yaina sau  kālaṅkatau taina rathaṃ paisaisiṃ| addasā khau, daiva, kumārau paitaṃ kālaṅkataṃ, disvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ panāyaṃ, samma sārathi, kālaṅkatau nāmā’’ti? ‘‘aisau khau, daiva, kālaṅkatau nāma| na dāni taṃ dakkhanti mātā vā pitā vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitā, saupi na dakkhissati mātaraṃ vā pitaraṃ vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, samma sārathi, ahampi maraṇadhammau maraṇaṃ anatītau; mampi na dakkhanti daivau vā daivī vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitā; ahampi na dakkhissāmi daivaṃ vā daiviṃ vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitai’’ti? ‘‘tvañ‍ca, daiva, mayañ‍camha sabbai maraṇadhammā maraṇaṃ anatītā; tampi na dakkhanti daivau vā daivī vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitā, tvampi na dakkhissasi daivaṃ vā daiviṃ vā añ‍ñai vā ñātisālauhitai’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samma sārathi, alaṃ dānaj‍ja uyyānabhūmiyā, itauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāhī’ti| ‘‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau ahaṃ, daiva, vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā tatauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāsiṃ| sau khau, daiva, kumārau antaipuraṃ gatau dukkhī dummanau pajjhāyati – ‘‘dhiratthu kira bhau jāti nāma, yatra hi nāma jātassa jarā pañ‍ñāyissati, byādhi pañ‍ñāyissati, maraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyissatī’’’ti|

pabbajitau

52. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumassa rañ‍ñau aitadahausi – ‘mā haiva khau vipassī kumārau na raj‍jaṃ kāraisi, mā haiva vipassī kumārau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji, mā  haiva naimittānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ assa vacana’nti| atha  khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumā rājā vipassissa kumārassa bhiyyausaumattāya pañ‍ca kāmaguṇāni upaṭṭhāpaisi – ‘yathā vipassī kumārau raj‍jaṃ karaiyya, yathā vipassī kumārau na agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya, yathā naimittānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ micchā assa vacana’nti|

‘‘tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bahūnaṃ vassānaṃ  bahūnaṃ vassasatānaṃ bahūnaṃ vassasahassānaṃ ac‍cayaina sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘yaujaihi, samma sārathi, bhaddāni bhaddāni yānāni, uyyānabhūmiṃ gacchāma subhūmidassanāyā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā bhaddāni bhaddāni yānāni yaujaitvā vipassissa kumārassa paṭivaidaisi – ‘yuttāni khau tai, daiva, bhaddāni bhaddāni yānāni, yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau bhaddaṃ bhaddaṃ yānaṃ abhiruhitvā bhaddaihi bhaddaihi yānaihi uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyāsi|

53. ‘‘addasā  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyantau purisaṃ bhaṇḍuṃ pabbajitaṃ kāsāyavasanaṃ| disvā sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘ayaṃ pana, samma sārathi, purisau kiṃkatau? sīsaṃpissa na yathā añ‍ñaisaṃ, vatthānipissa na yathā añ‍ñaisa’nti? ‘aisau khau, daiva, pabbajitau nāmā’ti| ‘kiṃ panaisau, samma sārathi, pabbajitau nāmā’ti? ‘aisau khau, daiva, pabbajitau nāma sādhu dhammacariyā sādhu samacariyā sammacariyā (ka॰) sādhu kusalakiriyā kusalacariyā (syā॰) sādhu puñ‍ñakiriyā sādhu avihiṃsā sādhu bhūtānukampā’ti| ‘sādhu khau sau, samma sārathi, pabbajitau nāma, sādhu  dhammacariyā sādhu samacariyā sādhu  kusalakiriyā sādhu puñ‍ñakiriyā sādhu avihiṃsā sādhu bhūtānukampā| taina hi, samma sārathi, yaina sau pabbajitau taina rathaṃ paisaihī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa kumārassa paṭissutvā yaina sau pabbajitau taina rathaṃ paisaisi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau taṃ pabbajitaṃ aitadavauca – ‘tvaṃ pana, samma, kiṃkatau, sīsampi tai na yathā añ‍ñaisaṃ, vatthānipi tai na yathā añ‍ñaisa’nti? ‘ahaṃ khau, daiva, pabbajitau nāmā’ti| ‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, samma, pabbajitau nāmā’ti? ‘ahaṃ khau, daiva, pabbajitau nāma, sādhu dhammacariyā sādhu samacariyā sādhu kusalakiriyā sādhu puñ‍ñakiriyā sādhu avihiṃsā sādhu bhūtānukampā’ti| ‘sādhu khau tvaṃ, samma, pabbajitau nāma sādhu dhammacariyā sādhu samacariyā sādhu kusalakiriyā sādhu puñ‍ñakiriyā sādhu avihiṃsā sādhu bhūtānukampā’ti|

baudhisattapabbaj‍jā

54. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī kumārau sārathiṃ āmantaisi – ‘taina hi, samma sārathi, rathaṃ ādāya itauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāhi| ahaṃ pana idhaiva kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sārathi vipassissa  kumārassa paṭissutvā rathaṃ ādāya tatauva antaipuraṃ pac‍caniyyāsi| vipassī pana kumārau tatthaiva kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji|

mahājanakāyaanupabbaj‍jā

55. ‘‘assausi khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā mahājanakāyau caturāsīti pāṇasahassāni – ‘vipassī kira kumārau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau’ti| sutvāna taisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘na hi nūna sau aurakau dhammavinayau, na sā aurakā aurikā (sī॰ syā॰) pabbaj‍jā, yattha vipassī kumārau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| vipassīpi nāma kumārau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissati, kimaṅgaṃ kimaṅga (sī॰) pana maya’nti|

‘‘atha khau, sau bhikkhavai, mahājanakāyau mahājanakāyau (syā॰) caturāsīti pāṇasahassāni kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā vipassiṃ baudhisattaṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitaṃ anupabbajiṃsu| tāya sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisāya parivutau vipassī baudhisattau gāmanigamajanapadarājadhānīsu cārikaṃ carati|

56. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘na khau maitaṃ na khau panaitaṃ (syā॰) patirūpaṃ yauhaṃ ākiṇṇau viharāmi, yaṃnūnāhaṃ aikau gaṇamhā vūpakaṭṭhau viharaiyya’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī baudhisattau aparaina samayaina aikau  gaṇamhā vūpakaṭṭhau vihāsi, añ‍ñainaiva tāni caturāsīti pabbajitasahassāni agamaṃsu, añ‍ñaina maggaina vipassī baudhisattau|

baudhisattaabhinivaisau

57. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa vāsūpagatassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘kicchaṃ vatāyaṃ laukau āpan‍nau, jāyati ca jīyati ca mīyati ca jiyyati ca miyyati ca (ka॰) cavati ca upapaj‍jati ca, atha ca panimassa dukkhassa  nissaraṇaṃ nappajānāti jarāmaraṇassa, kudāssu nāma imassa dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati jarāmaraṇassā’ti?

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘jātiyā khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’nti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jāti hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā jātī’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘bhavai khau sati jāti hauti, bhavapac‍cayā jātī’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati bhavau hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā bhavau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘upādānai khau sati bhavau hauti, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati upādānaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā  upādāna’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘taṇhāya khau sati upādānaṃ hauti, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādāna’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati taṇhā hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā taṇhā’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa  baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘vaidanāya khau sati taṇhā hauti, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati vaidanā hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā vaidanā’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa  yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘phassai khau sati vaidanā hauti, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati phassau hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā phassau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘saḷāyatanai khau sati phassau hauti, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati saḷāyatanaṃ  hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā saḷāyatana’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau sati saḷāyatanaṃ hauti, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatana’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā nāmarūpa’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa  baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpa’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, nāmarūpapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’nti|

58. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘pac‍cudāvattati khau idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpamhā, nāparaṃ gacchati| aittāvatā jāyaitha  vā jiyyaitha vā miyyaitha vā cavaitha vā upapaj‍jaitha vā, yadidaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’|

59. ‘‘‘samudayau samudayau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

60. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau  – ‘jātiyā khau asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati jāti na hauti, kissa niraudhā jātiniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘bhavai khau asati jāti na hauti, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati bhavau na hauti, kissa niraudhā bhavaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘upādānai khau asati bhavau na hauti, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati upādānaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā upādānaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu  pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘taṇhāya khau asati upādānaṃ na hauti, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati taṇhā na hauti, kissa niraudhā taṇhāniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau  – ‘vaidanāya khau asati taṇhā na hauti, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati vaidanā na hauti, kissa niraudhā vaidanāniraudhau’ti? atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘phassai khau asati vaidanā na hauti, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati phassau na hauti, kissa niraudhā phassaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘saḷāyatanai khau asati phassau na hauti, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau asati saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu  pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti, nāmarūpaniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’ti|

61. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘adhigatau  khau myāyaṃ maggau sambaudhāya  yadidaṃ – nāmarūpaniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti’|

62. ‘‘‘niraudhau niraudhau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

63. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī baudhisattau aparaina samayaina pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī vihāsi – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā, iti vaidanāya samudayau, iti vaidanāya atthaṅgamau; iti sañ‍ñā, iti sañ‍ñāya samudayau, iti sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau; iti saṅkhārā, iti saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau, iti saṅkhārānaṃ  atthaṅgamau; iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti, tassa pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassinau viharatau na cirassaiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cī’’ti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau|

brahmayācanakathā

64. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyya’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa  bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa  aitadahausi – ‘adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā| ālayarāmāya khau pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppādau| idampi khau ṭhānaṃ duddasaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, parai ca mai na ājānaiyyuṃ; sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’ti|

65. ‘‘apissu, bhikkhavai, vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ imā anacchariyā gāthāyau paṭibhaṃsu pubbai assutapubbā –

‘kicchaina mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ|

rāgadausaparaitaihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘paṭisautagāmiṃ nipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ|

rāgarattā na dakkhanti, tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā’ti||

‘‘itiha, bhikkhavai, vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa paṭisañ‍cikkhatau appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ nami, nau dhammadaisanāya|

66. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarassa mahābrahmunau vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya aitadahausi  – ‘nassati vata bhau laukau, vinassati vata bhau laukau, yatra hi nāma vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati nami (syā॰ ka॰), namissati (?), nau dhammadaisanāyā’ti| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, mahābrahmā saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya; aivamaiva brahmalaukai antarahitau vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa puratau pāturahausi| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, mahābrahmā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā dakkhiṇaṃ jāṇumaṇḍalaṃ pathaviyaṃ  nihantvā nidahantau (syā॰) yaina vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘daisaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau dhammaṃ, santi santī (syā॰) sattā apparajakkhajātikā; assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’ti|

67. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai atha khau (ka॰), bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau taṃ mahābrahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘mayhampi khau, brahmai, aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyya’’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, brahmai, aitadahausi – ‘‘adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā| ālayarāmāya khau pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya  duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppādau| idampi khau ṭhānaṃ duddasaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, parai ca mai na ājānaiyyuṃ; sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’’ti| apissu maṃ, brahmai, imā anacchariyā gāthāyau paṭibhaṃsu pubbai assutapubbā –

‘‘kicchaina mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ|

rāgadausaparaitaihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘‘paṭisautagāmiṃ nipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ|

rāgarattā na dakkhanti, tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā’’ti||

‘itiha  mai, brahmai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ nami, nau dhammadaisanāyā’ti|

68. ‘‘dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, sau mahābrahmā…pai॰… tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, sau mahābrahmā vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘daisaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau dhammaṃ, santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’ti|

69. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau brahmunau ca ajjhaisanaṃ viditvā sattaisu ca kāruñ‍ñataṃ paṭic‍ca buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaisi| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai bhabbai abhabbai (syā॰) appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai dassāvinau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai dassāvinau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) viharantai| saiyyathāpi nāma uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ  vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antau nimuggapausīni| appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni samaudakaṃ ṭhitāni| appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakā ac‍cuggamma ṭhitāni anupalittāni udakaina| aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau addasa sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai|

70. ‘‘atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, mahābrahmā vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘sailai yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau, yathāpi passai janataṃ samantatau|

tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumaidha, pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu||

‘saukāvatiṇṇaṃ  saukāvakiṇṇaṃ (syā॰) janatamapaitasaukau,

avaikkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ|

uṭṭhaihi  vīra vijitasaṅgāma,

satthavāha aṇaṇa vicara laukai||

daisassu daisaitu (syā॰ pī॰) bhagavā dhammaṃ,

añ‍ñātārau bhavissantī’ti||

71. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau taṃ mahābrahmānaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘apārutā taisaṃ amatassa dvārā,

yai sautavantau pamuñ‍cantu saddhaṃ|

vihiṃsasañ‍ñī paguṇaṃ na bhāsiṃ,

dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujaisu brahmai’ti||

‘‘atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, mahābrahmā ‘katāvakāsau khaumhi vipassinā bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dhammadaisanāyā’ti vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ  sammāsambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaiva antaradhāyi|

aggasāvakayugaṃ

72. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aitadahausi – ‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, kau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau khaṇḍau ca rājaputtau tissau ca purauhitaputtau bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā paṭivasanti paṇḍitā viyattā maidhāvinau dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhajātikā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ khaṇḍassa ca rājaputtassa, tissassa ca purauhitaputtassa paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, tai imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissantī’ti|

73. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau saiyyathāpi nāma balavā  purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya; aivamaiva baudhirukkhamūlai antarahitau bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā khaimai migadāyai pāturahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dāyapālaṃ migadāyapālaṃ (syā॰) āmantaisi – ‘aihi tvaṃ, samma dāyapāla, bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ pavisitvā khaṇḍañ‍ca rājaputtaṃ tissañ‍ca purauhitaputtaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – vipassī, bhantai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ anuppattau khaimai migadāyai viharati, sau  tumhākaṃ dassanakāmau’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’ti khau, bhikkhavai, dāyapālau vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa paṭissutvā bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ pavisitvā khaṇḍañ‍ca rājaputtaṃ tissañ‍ca purauhitaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘vipassī, bhantai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ anuppattau khaimai migadāyai viharati; sau tumhākaṃ dassanakāmau’ti|

74. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, khaṇḍau ca rājaputtau tissau ca  purauhitaputtau bhaddāni bhaddāni yānāni yaujāpaitvā bhaddaṃ bhaddaṃ yānaṃ abhiruhitvā bhaddaihi bhaddaihi yānaihi bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā niyyiṃsu| yaina khaimau migadāyau taina pāyiṃsu| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikāva padikāva (syā॰) yaina vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| upasaṅkamitvā vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ  sammāsambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

75. ‘‘taisaṃ vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau anupubbiṃ kathaṃ ānupubbikathaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā tai bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittai muducittai vinīvaraṇacittai udaggacittai pasan‍nacittai, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva khaṇḍassa ca rājaputtassa tissassa ca purauhitaputtassa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’nti|

76. ‘‘tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai  vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’’ti| aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau  pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ  gacchāma dhammañ‍ca| labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’nti|

77. ‘‘alatthuṃ khau, bhikkhavai, khaṇḍau ca rājaputtau, tissau ca purauhitaputtau vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ alatthuṃ upasampadaṃ| tai vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi; saṅkhārānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ nibbānai naikkhammai (syā॰) ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| taisaṃ vipassinā bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassiyamānānaṃ samādapiyamānānaṃ samuttaijiyamānānaṃ sampahaṃsiyamānānaṃ nacirassaiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu|

mahājanakāyapabbaj‍jā

78. ‘‘assausi khau, bhikkhavai, bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā mahājanakāyau caturāsītipāṇasahassāni – ‘vipassī kira bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ anuppattau khaimai migadāyai viharati| khaṇḍau ca kira rājaputtau tissau ca purauhitaputtau vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa santikai kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā’ti| sutvāna naisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘na hi nūna sau aurakau dhammavinayau, na sā aurakā pabbaj‍jā, yattha khaṇḍau ca rājaputtau tissau ca purauhitaputtau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā| khaṇḍau ca rājaputtau tissau ca purauhitaputtau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissanti, kimaṅgaṃ pana maya’nti| atha  khau sau, bhikkhavai, mahājanakāyau caturāsītipāṇasahassāni bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā nikkhamitvā yaina khaimau migadāyau yaina vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau  tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

79. ‘‘taisaṃ  vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi| saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā tai bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittai muducittai vinīvaraṇacittai udaggacittai pasan‍nacittai, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva taisaṃ caturāsītipāṇasahassānaṃ tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’nti|

80. ‘‘tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’’ti| aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca ( ) natthi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ, pāḷiyaṃ pana sabbatthapi dissati| labhaiyyāma  mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti|

81. ‘‘alatthuṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tāni caturāsītipāṇasahassāni vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alatthuṃ upasampadaṃ| tai vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi; saṅkhārānaṃ  ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ nibbānai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| taisaṃ vipassinā bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassiyamānānaṃ samādapiyamānānaṃ samuttaijiyamānānaṃ sampahaṃsiyamānānaṃ nacirassaiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu|

purimapabbajitānaṃ dhammābhisamayau

82. ‘‘assausuṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tāni purimāni caturāsītipabbajitasahassāni – ‘vipassī kira bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ  anuppattau khaimai migadāyai viharati, dhammañ‍ca kira daisaitī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tāni caturāsītipabbajitasahassāni yaina bandhumatī rājadhānī yaina khaimau migadāyau yaina vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

83. ‘‘taisaṃ  vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi| saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā tai bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittai muducittai  vinīvaraṇacittai udaggacittai pasan‍nacittai, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva taisaṃ caturāsītipabbajitasahassānaṃ tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’nti|

84. ‘‘tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’’ti| aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti|

85. ‘‘alatthuṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tāni caturāsītipabbajitasahassāni vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ alatthuṃ upasampadaṃ| tai vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi; saṅkhārānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ nibbānai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| taisaṃ vipassinā bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassiyamānānaṃ  samādapiyamānānaṃ  samuttaijiyamānānaṃ sampahaṃsiyamānānaṃ nacirassaiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu|

cārikāanujānanaṃ

86. ‘‘taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā mahābhikkhusaṅghau paṭivasati aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘mahā khau aitarahi bhikkhusaṅghau bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā paṭivasati aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ, yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhū anujānaiyyaṃ – ‘caratha, bhikkhavai, cārikaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya  atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ; mā aikaina dvai agamittha; daisaitha, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitha| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau| api ca chan‍naṃ chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina bandhumatī rājadhānī upasaṅkamitabbā pātimaukkhuddaisāyā’’’ti|

87. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau mahābrahmā vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya| aivamaiva brahmalaukai antarahitau vipassissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa puratau  pāturahausi| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, mahābrahmā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ, sugata| mahā khau, bhantai, aitarahi bhikkhusaṅghau bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā paṭivasati aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ, anujānātu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhū – ‘‘caratha, bhikkhavai, cārikaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ; mā aikaina dvai agamittha; daisaitha, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ ādikalyāṇaṃ  majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitha| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti añ‍ñātārau (sabbattha)| api ca, bhantai, mayaṃ tathā karissāma yathā bhikkhū chan‍naṃ chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ upasaṅkamissanti pātimaukkhuddaisāyā’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sau mahābrahmā, idaṃ vatvā vipassiṃ  bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaiva antaradhāyi|

88. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘idha mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – mahā khau aitarahi bhikkhusaṅghau bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā paṭivasati aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhū anujānaiyyaṃ – ‘caratha, bhikkhavai, cārikaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ; mā aikaina dvai agamittha; daisaitha, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitha| santi sattā  apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau| api ca, chan‍naṃ chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina bandhumatī rājadhānī upasaṅkamitabbā pātimaukkhuddaisāyāti|

‘‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau mahābrahmā mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva brahmalaukai antarahitau mama puratau pāturahausi| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, mahābrahmā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaināhaṃ tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ, sugata| mahā khau, bhantai, aitarahi bhikkhusaṅghau bandhumatiyā rājadhāniyā paṭivasati aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ| anujānātu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhū – ‘caratha, bhikkhavai, cārikaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya  sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ; mā aikaina dvai agamittha; daisaitha, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ…pai॰… santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’ti| api  ca, bhantai, mayaṃ tathā karissāma, yathā bhikkhū chan‍naṃ chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ upasaṅkamissanti pātimaukkhuddaisāyā’’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sau mahābrahmā, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaiva antaradhāyi’|

‘‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, caratha cārikaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ; mā aikaina dvai agamittha; daisaitha, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitha| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau| api ca, bhikkhavai, chan‍naṃ chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina bandhumatī rājadhānī upasaṅkamitabbā pātimaukkhuddaisāyā’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yaibhuyyaina aikāhainaiva janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu|

89. ‘‘taina khau pana samayaina jambudīpai caturāsīti āvāsasahassāni haunti| aikamhi hi vassai nikkhantai daivatā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘nikkhantaṃ khau, mārisā, aikaṃ vassaṃ; pañ‍ca dāni vassāni saisāni; pañ‍can‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina bandhumatī rājadhānī upasaṅkamitabbā pātimaukkhuddaisāyā’ti| dvīsu vassaisu nikkhantaisu…  tīsu vassaisu nikkhantaisu… catūsu vassaisu nikkhantaisu… pañ‍casu vassaisu nikkhantaisu daivatā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘nikkhantāni khau, mārisā, pañ‍cavassāni; aikaṃ dāni vassaṃ saisaṃ; aikassa vassassa ac‍cayaina bandhumatī rājadhānī upasaṅkamitabbā pātimaukkhuddaisāyā’ti| chasu vassaisu nikkhantaisu daivatā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘nikkhantāni khau, mārisā, chabbassāni, samayau dāni bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ upasaṅkamituṃ pātimaukkhuddaisāyā’ti| atha khau tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū appaikac‍cai sakaina iddhānubhāvaina appaikac‍cai daivatānaṃ iddhānubhāvaina aikāhainaiva bandhumatiṃ rājadhāniṃ upasaṅkamiṃsu pātimaukkhuddaisāyāti pātimaukkhuddaisāya (?)|

90. ‘‘tatra  sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhikkhusaṅghai aivaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati –

‘khantī  paramaṃ tapau titikkhā,

nibbānaṃ paramaṃ vadanti buddhā|

na hi pabbajitau parūpaghātī,

na samaṇau samaṇau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) hauti paraṃ vihaiṭhayantau||

‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudapanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhānasāsanaṃ||

‘anūpavādau anūpaghātau anupavādau anupaghātau (pī॰ ka॰), pātimaukkhai ca saṃvarau|

mattañ‍ñutā  ca bhattasmiṃ, pantañ‍ca sayanāsanaṃ|

adhicittai ca āyaugau, aitaṃ buddhānasāsana’nti||

daivatāraucanaṃ

91. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, samayaṃ uk‍kaṭṭhāyaṃ viharāmi subhagavanai sālarājamūlai| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘na khau sau sattāvāsau sulabharūpau, yau mayā anāvutthapubbau anajjhāvuṭṭhapubbau (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰) iminā dīghaina addhunā añ‍ñatra suddhāvāsaihi daivaihi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina suddhāvāsā daivā tainupasaṅkamaiyya’nti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva uk‍kaṭṭhāyaṃ subhagavanai sālarājamūlai antarahitau avihaisu daivaisu pāturahausiṃ| tasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, daivanikāyai anaikāni daivatāsahassāni anaikāni daivatāsatasahassāni anaikāni daivatāsatāni anaikāni daivatāsahassāni (syā॰) yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, tā daivatā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘itau sau, mārisā, aikanavutikappai yaṃ vipassī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai udapādi| vipassī, mārisā, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau khattiyau jātiyā ahausi, khattiyakulai udapādi| vipassī, mārisā, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kauṇḍañ‍ñau gauttaina ahausi| vipassissa, mārisā, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa asītivassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| vipassī, mārisā, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pāṭaliyā mūlai abhisambuddhau| vipassissa, mārisā, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa khaṇḍatissaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| vipassissa, mārisā, bhagavatau  arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ| aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi aṭṭhasaṭṭhibhikkhusatasahassaṃ| aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi bhikkhusatasahassaṃ| aikau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau ahausi asītibhikkhusahassāni| vipassissa, mārisā, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa imai tayau sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātā ahaisuṃ sabbaisaṃyaiva khīṇāsavānaṃ| vipassissa, mārisā, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa asaukau nāma bhikkhu upaṭṭhākau ahausi aggupaṭṭhākau| vipassissa, mārisa, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa bandhumā nāma rājā pitā ahausi| bandhumatī nāma daivī mātā ahausi janaitti| bandhumassa rañ‍ñau bandhumatī nāma nagaraṃ rājadhānī ahausi| vipassissa, mārisā, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa aivaṃ abhinikkhamanaṃ ahausi aivaṃ pabbaj‍jā aivaṃ padhānaṃ aivaṃ abhisambaudhi aivaṃ dhammacak‍kappavattanaṃ| tai mayaṃ, mārisā, vipassimhi bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ caritvā kāmaisu kāmacchandaṃ virājaitvā idhūpapan‍nā’ti …pai॰…
