165. ‘‘kiṃ  panānanda, bhikkhusaṅghau mayi pac‍cāsīsati pac‍cāsiṃsati (sī॰ syā॰)? daisitau, ānanda, mayā dhammau anantaraṃ abāhiraṃ karitvā| natthānanda, tathāgatassa dhammaisu ācariyamuṭṭhi| yassa nūna, ānanda, aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’ti vā ‘mamuddaisikau bhikkhusaṅghau’ti vā, sau nūna, ānanda, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ ārabbha kiñ‍cidaiva udāharaiyya| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’ti vā ‘mamuddaisikau bhikkhusaṅghau’ti vā| sakiṃ kiṃ (sī॰ pī॰), ānanda, tathāgatau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ ārabbha kiñ‍cidaiva udāharissati| ahaṃ khau panānanda, aitarahi jiṇṇau vuddhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| āsītikau mai vayau vattati| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, jaj‍jarasakaṭaṃ vaiṭhamissakaina vaiḷumissakaina (syā॰), vaighamissakaina (pī॰), vaidhamissakaina, vaikhamissakaina (ka॰) yāpaiti, aivamaiva khau, ānanda, vaiṭhamissakaina mañ‍ñai tathāgatassa kāyau yāpaiti| yasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatau sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā aikac‍cānaṃ vaidanānaṃ niraudhā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, phāsutarau, ānanda, tasmiṃ samayai tathāgatassa  kāyau hauti| tasmātihānanda, attadīpā viharatha attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā| kathañ‍cānanda, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau? idhānanda, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau| yai  hi kaici, ānanda, aitarahi vā mama vā ac‍cayaina attadīpā viharissanti attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, tamataggai mai tai, ānanda, bhikkhū bhavissanti yai kaici sikkhākāmā’’ti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau|

nimittaubhāsakathā

166. atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaṇhāhi, ānanda, nisīdanaṃ, yaina cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ pāvālaṃ (caitiyaṃ (syā॰) tainupasaṅkamissāma divā vihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā nisīdanaṃ ādāya bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| atha  khau bhagavā yaina cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau ānandau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

167. aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ gautamakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sattambaṃ sattambakaṃ (pī॰) caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ bahuputtaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa  kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau ākaṅkhamānau (?), ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’’ti| aivampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatā auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ; na bhagavantaṃ yāci – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti, yathā taṃ māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittau| dutiyampi  khau bhagavā…pai॰… tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ gautamakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sattambaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ bahuputtaṃ  caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’’ti| aivampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatā auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ; na bhagavantaṃ yāci – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti, yathā taṃ māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittau| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, ānanda, yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi|

mārayācanakathā

168. atha khau mārau pāpimā acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai ānandai yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau mārau pāpimā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālau  dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānī uttāniṃ (ka॰), uttāni (sī॰ pī॰) karissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’ti| aitarahi khau pana, bhantai, bhikkhū bhagavatau sāvakā viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā  sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti| parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau|

‘‘bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai bhikkhuniyau na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’ti| aitarahi khau pana, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau bhagavatau sāvikā viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti| parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau|

‘‘bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai upāsakā na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’ti| aitarahi khau pana, bhantai, upāsakā bhagavatau sāvakā viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti| parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau|

‘‘bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai upāsikā na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’ti| aitarahi khau pana, bhantai, upāsikā bhagavatau sāvikā viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ  uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti| parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau|

‘‘bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na iddhaṃ caiva bhavissati phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsita’nti| aitarahi khau pana, bhantai, bhagavatau brahmacariyaṃ iddhaṃ caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ, yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘appaussuk‍kau tvaṃ, pāpima, hauhi, na ciraṃ tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina tathāgatau parinibbāyissatī’’ti|

āyusaṅkhāraaussaj‍janaṃ

169. atha khau bhagavā cāpālai caitiyai satau sampajānau āyusaṅkhāraṃ aussaji| aussaṭṭhai ca bhagavatā āyusaṅkhārai mahābhūmicālau ahausi bhiṃsanakau salaumahaṃsau laumahaṃsau (syā॰), daivadundubhiyau daivadudrabhiyau (ka॰) ca phaliṃsu| atha khau bhagavā  aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘tulamatulañ‍ca sambhavaṃ, bhavasaṅkhāramavassaji muni|

ajjhattaratau samāhitau, abhindi kavacamivattasambhava’’nti||

mahābhūmicālahaitu

170. atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, mahā vatāyaṃ bhūmicālau; sumahā vatāyaṃ bhūmicālau bhiṃsanakau salaumahaṃsau; daivadundubhiyau ca phaliṃsu| kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā’’ti?

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi, aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai, mahā vatāyaṃ, bhantai, bhūmicālau; sumahā vatāyaṃ, bhantai, bhūmicālau bhiṃsanakau salaumahaṃsau; daivadundubhiyau ca phaliṃsu| kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā’’ti?

171. ‘‘aṭṭha khau imai, ānanda, haitū, aṭṭha pac‍cayā mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya| katamai aṭṭha? ayaṃ, ānanda, mahāpathavī udakai patiṭṭhitā, udakaṃ vātai patiṭṭhitaṃ, vātau ākāsaṭṭhau| hauti khau sau, ānanda, samayau, yaṃ mahāvātā vāyanti| mahāvātā vāyantā udakaṃ kampainti| udakaṃ kampitaṃ pathaviṃ kampaiti| ayaṃ paṭhamau  haitu paṭhamau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, samaṇau vā hauti brāhmaṇau vā iddhimā caitauvasippattau, daivau vā mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau, tassa parittā pathavīsañ‍ñā bhāvitā hauti, appamāṇā āpausañ‍ñā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ kampaiti saṅkampaiti sampakampaiti sampavaidhaiti| ayaṃ dutiyau haitu dutiyau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā baudhisattau tusitakāyā cavitvā satau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ tatiyau haitu tatiyau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā baudhisattau satau sampajānau mātukucchismā nikkhamati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ catutthau haitu catutthau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā  tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ pañ‍camau haitu pañ‍camau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā tathāgatau anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau haitu chaṭṭhau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā tathāgatau satau sampajānau āyusaṅkhāraṃ aussaj‍jati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ sattamau haitu sattamau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā tathāgatau anupādisaisāya  nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau haitu aṭṭhamau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya| imai khau, ānanda, aṭṭha haitū, aṭṭha pac‍cayā mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā’’ti|

aṭṭha parisā

172. ‘‘aṭṭha khau imā, ānanda, parisā| katamā aṭṭha? khattiyaparisā, brāhmaṇaparisā, gahapatiparisā, samaṇaparisā, cātumahārājikaparisā cātummahārājikaparisā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), tāvatiṃsaparisā, māraparisā, brahmaparisā| abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ, ānanda, anaikasataṃ khattiyaparisaṃ upasaṅkamitā| tatrapi mayā san‍nisin‍napubbaṃ caiva sal‍lapitapubbañ‍ca sākacchā ca samāpaj‍jitapubbā| tattha yādisakau taisaṃ vaṇṇau hauti, tādisakau mayhaṃ vaṇṇau hauti| yādisakau taisaṃ sarau hauti, tādisakau mayhaṃ sarau hauti| dhammiyā kathāya sandassaimi samādapaimi samuttaijaimi sampahaṃsaimi| bhāsamānañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu khau ayaṃ bhāsati daivau vā manussau vā’ti? dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā antaradhāyāmi| antarahitañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu khau ayaṃ antarahitau daivau vā manussau vā’ti? abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ, ānanda, anaikasataṃ brāhmaṇaparisaṃ…pai॰… gahapatiparisaṃ… samaṇaparisaṃ… cātumahārājikaparisaṃ… tāvatiṃsaparisaṃ… māraparisaṃ… brahmaparisaṃ upasaṅkamitā| tatrapi mayā san‍nisin‍napubbaṃ caiva sal‍lapitapubbañ‍ca sākacchā ca samāpaj‍jitapubbā| tattha yādisakau taisaṃ vaṇṇau hauti, tādisakau mayhaṃ vaṇṇau hauti| yādisakau taisaṃ sarau hauti, tādisakau mayhaṃ sarau hauti| dhammiyā kathāya sandassaimi samādapaimi samuttaijaimi sampahaṃsaimi| bhāsamānañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu khau ayaṃ bhāsati daivau vā manussau vā’ti? dhammiyā  kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā antaradhāyāmi| antarahitañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu khau ayaṃ antarahitau daivau vā manussau vā’ti? imā khau, ānanda, aṭṭha parisā|

aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni

173. ‘‘aṭṭha  khau imāni, ānanda, abhibhāyatanāni| katamāni aṭṭha? ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ dutiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma umāpupphaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ| saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma kaṇikārapupphaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ| saiyyathā  vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma bandhujīvakapupphaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ| saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma ausadhitārakā  audātā audātavaṇṇā audātanidassanā audātanibhāsā| saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ audātaṃ audātavaṇṇaṃ audātanidassanaṃ audātanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ| imāni khau, ānanda, aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni|

aṭṭha vimaukkhā

174. ‘‘aṭṭha khau imai, ānanda, vimaukkhā| katamai aṭṭha? rūpī rūpāni passati, ayaṃ paṭhamau vimaukkhau| ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati, ayaṃ dutiyau vimaukkhau| subhantaiva adhimuttau hauti, ayaṃ tatiyau vimaukkhau| sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ catutthau vimaukkhau| sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ pañ‍camau vimaukkhau| sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ chaṭṭhau vimaukkhau| sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ sattamau vimaukkhau| sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ aṭṭhamau vimaukkhau| imai khau, ānanda, aṭṭha vimaukkhā|

175. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, ānanda, samayaṃ uruvailāyaṃ viharāmi naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| atha khau, ānanda, mārau pāpimā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, ānanda, mārau pāpimā maṃ  aitadavauca – ‘parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā; parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau’ti| aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, ānanda, māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ –

‘‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā  sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti  vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissanti|

‘‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai bhikkhuniyau na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina  suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissanti|

‘‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai upāsakā na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissanti|

‘‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai upāsikā na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissanti|

‘‘‘na  tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na iddhañ‍caiva bhavissati phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsita’nti|

176. ‘‘idānaiva khau, ānanda, aj‍ja cāpālai caitiyai mārau pāpimā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, ānanda, mārau pāpimā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi, yāva mai bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti…pai॰… yāva mai bhikkhuniyau na sāvikā bhavissanti…pai॰… yāva mai upāsakā na sāvakā bhavissanti…pai॰… yāva mai upāsikā na sāvikā bhavissanti…pai॰… yāva mai idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na iddhañ‍caiva bhavissati phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ, yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsita’’nti| aitarahi khau pana, bhantai, bhagavatau brahmacariyaṃ  iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ, yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| parinibbātudāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau, parinibbānakālaudāni, bhantai, bhagavatau’ti|

177. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, ānanda, māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘appaussuk‍kau tvaṃ, pāpima, hauhi, naciraṃ tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina tathāgatau parinibbāyissatī’ti| idānaiva khau, ānanda, aj‍ja cāpālai caitiyai tathāgataina sataina sampajānaina āyusaṅkhārau aussaṭṭhau’’ti|

ānandayācanakathā

178. aivaṃ  vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti|

‘‘alaṃdāni, ānanda| mā tathāgataṃ yāci, akālaudāni, ānanda, tathāgataṃ yācanāyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti|

‘‘saddahasi  tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatassa baudhi’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘atha kiñ‍carahi tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataṃ yāvatatiyakaṃ abhinippīḷaisī’’ti? ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau  kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| sau  ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’’’ti| ‘‘saddahasi tvaṃ, ānandā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātihānanda, tuyhaivaitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, tuyhaivaitaṃ aparaddhaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ tathāgataina aivaṃ auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ, na tathāgataṃ yāci – ‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti| sacai tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataṃ yācaiyyāsi, dvaiva tai vācā tathāgatau paṭikkhipaiyya, atha tatiyakaṃ adhivāsaiyya| tasmātihānanda, tuyhaivaitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, tuyhaivaitaṃ aparaddhaṃ|

179. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, ānanda, samayaṃ rājagahai viharāmi gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| tatrāpi khau tāhaṃ, ānanda, āmantaisiṃ  – ‘ramaṇīyaṃ, ānanda, rājagahaṃ, ramaṇīyau, ānanda, gijjhakūṭau pabbatau| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya  kappāvasaisaṃ vā’ti| aivampi khau tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataina auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ, na tathāgataṃ yāci – ‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’nti| sacai tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataṃ yācaiyyāsi, dvai tai vācā tathāgatau paṭikkhipaiyya, atha tatiyakaṃ adhivāsaiyya| tasmātihānanda, tuyhaivaitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, tuyhaivaitaṃ aparaddhaṃ|

180. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, ānanda, samayaṃ tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi gautamanigraudhai…pai॰… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi caurapapātai… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi vaibhārapassai sattapaṇṇiguhāyaṃ… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi isigilipassai kāḷasilāyaṃ… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi  sītavanai sappasauṇḍikapabbhārai… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi tapaudārāmai… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi jīvakambavanai… tatthaiva rājagahai viharāmi maddakucchismiṃ migadāyai  tatrāpi khau tāhaṃ, ānanda, āmantaisiṃ – ‘ramaṇīyaṃ, ānanda, rājagahaṃ, ramaṇīyau gijjhakūṭau pabbatau, ramaṇīyau gautamanigraudhau, ramaṇīyau caurapapātau, ramaṇīyā vaibhārapassai sattapaṇṇiguhā, ramaṇīyā isigilipassai kāḷasilā, ramaṇīyau sītavanai sappasauṇḍikapabbhārau, ramaṇīyau tapaudārāmau, ramaṇīyau vaiḷuvanai  kalandakanivāpau, ramaṇīyaṃ jīvakambavanaṃ, ramaṇīyau maddakucchismiṃ migadāyau| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā…pai॰… ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’ti| aivampi khau tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataina auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ, na tathāgataṃ yāci – ‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’nti| sacai tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataṃ yācaiyyāsi, dvaiva tai vācā tathāgatau paṭikkhipaiyya, atha tatiyakaṃ adhivāsaiyya| tasmātihānanda, tuyhaivaitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, tuyhaivaitaṃ aparaddhaṃ|

181. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, ānanda, samayaṃ idhaiva vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi udainai caitiyai| tatrāpi khau tāhaṃ, ānanda, āmantaisiṃ – ‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā  bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’ti| aivampi khau tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataina auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ, na tathāgataṃ yāci – ‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’nti| sacai tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataṃ yācaiyyāsi, dvaiva tai vācā tathāgatau paṭikkhipaiyya, atha tatiyakaṃ adhivāsaiyya, tasmātihānanda, tuyhaivaitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, tuyhaivaitaṃ aparaddhaṃ|

182. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, ānanda, samayaṃ idhaiva vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi gautamakai caitiyai  …pai॰… idhaiva vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi sattambai caitiyai… idhaiva vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi bahuputtai caitiyai… idhaiva vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi sārandadai caitiyai… idānaiva khau tāhaṃ, ānanda, aj‍ja cāpālai caitiyai āmantaisiṃ – ‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ gautamakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sattambaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ bahuputtaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā  anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’ti| aivampi khau tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataina auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ, na tathāgataṃ yāci – ‘tiṭṭhatu bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’nti| sacai tvaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataṃ yācaiyyāsi, dvaiva tai vācā tathāgatau paṭikkhipaiyya, atha tatiyakaṃ adhivāsaiyya| tasmātihānanda, tuyhaivaitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, tuyhaivaitaṃ aparaddhaṃ|

183. ‘‘nanu aitaṃ aivaṃ (syā॰ pī॰), ānanda, mayā paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰ pī॰) akkhātaṃ – ‘sabbaihaiva piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau añ‍ñathābhāvau| taṃ kutaittha, ānanda, labbhā, yaṃ taṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palaukadhammaṃ, taṃ vata mā paluj‍jīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| yaṃ khau panaitaṃ, ānanda, tathāgataina cattaṃ vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ aussaṭṭhau āyusaṅkhārau, aikaṃsaina vācā bhāsitā – ‘na ciraṃ tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ  bhavissati| itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina tathāgatau parinibbāyissatī’ti| tañ‍ca taṃ vacanaṃ (sī॰) tathāgatau jīvitahaitu puna pac‍cāvamissatīti pac‍cāgamissatīti (syā॰ ka॰) naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| āyāmānanda, yaina mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau  āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau bhagavā  āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ yaina mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, ānanda, yāvatikā bhikkhū vaisāliṃ upanissāya viharanti, tai sabbai upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nipātaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yāvatikā bhikkhū vaisāliṃ upanissāya viharanti, tai sabbai upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nipātaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘san‍nipatitau, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghau, yassadāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

184. atha khau bhagavā yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yai tai mayā dhammā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, tai vau sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā āsaivitabbā bhāvaitabbā bahulīkātabbā, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai ca tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā mayā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, yai vau sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā āsaivitabbā bhāvaitabbā bahulīkātabbā, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ  addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya  daivamanussānaṃ| saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau iddhipādā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| imai khau tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā mayā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, yai vau sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā āsaivitabbā bhāvaitabbā bahulīkātabbā, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti|

185. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘handadāni, bhikkhavai, āmantayāmi vau, vayadhammā saṅkhārā, appamādaina sampādaitha| naciraṃ tathāgatassa  parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina tathāgatau parinibbāyissatī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā itau paraṃ syāmapautthakai aivaṃpi pāṭhau dissati –§daharāpi ca yai vuddhā, yai bālā yai ca paṇḍitā||§aḍḍhācaiva daliddā ca, sabbai mac‍cuparāyanā|§yathāpi kumbhakārassa, kataṃ mattikabhājanaṃ||§khuddakañ‍ca mahantañ‍ca, yañ‍ca pak‍kaṃ yañ‍ca āmakaṃ|§sabbaṃ bhaidapariyantaṃ, aivaṃ mac‍cāna jīvitaṃ||§athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā| –

‘‘paripak‍kau  vayau mayhaṃ, parittaṃ mama jīvitaṃ|

pahāya vau gamissāmi, kataṃ mai saraṇamattanau||

‘‘appamattā satīmantau, susīlā hautha bhikkhavau|

susamāhitasaṅkappā, sacittamanurakkhatha||

‘‘yau  imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, appamattau vihassati|

pahāya jātisaṃsāraṃ, dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’’ti viharissati (syā॰), vihaissati (sī॰)||

tatiyau bhāṇavārau|

nāgāpalaukitaṃ

186. atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau nāgāpalaukitaṃ vaisāliṃ apalaukaitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘idaṃ pacchimakaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatassa vaisāliyā dassanaṃ bhavissati| āyāmānanda, yaina bhaṇḍagāmau bhaṇḍugāmau (ka॰) tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

atha  khau bhagavā mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina bhaṇḍagāmau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bhaṇḍagāmai viharati| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? ariyassa, bhikkhavai, sīlassa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamaṃ caiva tumhākañ‍ca| ariyassa, bhikkhavai, samādhissa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ  mamaṃ caiva tumhākañ‍ca| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāya ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamaṃ caiva tumhākañ‍ca| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, vimuttiyā ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamaṃ caiva tumhākañ‍ca| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ sīlaṃ anubuddhaṃ  paṭividdhaṃ, ariyau samādhi anubuddhau paṭividdhau, ariyā pañ‍ñā anubuddhā paṭividdhā, ariyā vimutti anubuddhā paṭividdhā, ucchin‍nā bhavataṇhā, khīṇā bhavanaitti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘sīlaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñā ca, vimutti ca anuttarā|

anubuddhā imai dhammā, gautamaina yasassinā||

‘‘iti  buddhau abhiñ‍ñāya, dhammamakkhāsi bhikkhunaṃ|

dukkhassantakarau satthā, cakkhumā parinibbutau’’ti||

tatrāpi sudaṃ bhagavā bhaṇḍagāmai viharantau aitadaiva bahulaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karauti – ‘‘iti sīlaṃ, iti samādhi, iti pañ‍ñā| sīlaparibhāvitau samādhi mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau| samādhiparibhāvitā pañ‍ñā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| pañ‍ñāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ sammadaiva āsavaihi vimuc‍cati, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmāsavā, bhavāsavā, avij‍jāsavā’’ti|

catumahāpadaisakathā

187. atha  khau bhagavā bhaṇḍagāmai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina hatthigāmau, yaina ambagāmau, yaina jambugāmau, yaina bhauganagaraṃ tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau  bhagavā mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina bhauganagaraṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bhauganagarai viharati ānandai sānandarai (ka॰) caitiyai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, mahāpadaisai daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasikarautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

188. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ, ayaṃ dhammau ayaṃ vinayau idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai ausāraitabbāni autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai ausāriyamānāni autāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni na caiva suttai ausaranti autaranti (sī॰ pī॰ a॰ ni॰ 4.180, na ca vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; imassa ca bhikkhunau duggahita’nti| itihaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha| tāni cai suttai ausāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni suttai caiva  ausaranti, vinayai ca sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; imassa ca bhikkhunau suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai saṅghau viharati sathairau sapāmaukkhau| tassa mai saṅghassa sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā  paṭiggahitaṃ, ayaṃ dhammau ayaṃ vinayau idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai ausāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai ausāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni na caiva suttai ausaranti, na ca vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; tassa ca saṅghassa duggahita’nti| itihaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha| tāni cai suttai ausāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni suttai caiva  ausaranti vinayai ca sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; tassa ca saṅghassa suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai sambahulā thairā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā| taisaṃ mai thairānaṃ sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau ayaṃ vinayau idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ…pai॰… na ca vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; taisañ‍ca thairānaṃ duggahita’nti| itihaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha| tāni cai suttai ausāriyamānāni…pai॰… vinayai ca sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; taisañ‍ca thairānaṃ suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai aikau thairau bhikkhu viharati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau| tassa mai thairassa sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau ayaṃ vinayau idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai ausāritabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai ausāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni na caiva suttai ausaranti, na ca vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; tassa ca thairassa duggahita’nti| itihaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha| tāni ca suttai ausāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni suttai caiva ausaranti, vinayai ca sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ; tassa ca thairassa suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau mahāpadaisai dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

tatrapi sudaṃ bhagavā bhauganagarai viharantau  ānandai caitiyai aitadaiva bahulaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karauti – ‘‘iti sīlaṃ, iti samādhi, iti pañ‍ñā| sīlaparibhāvitau samādhi mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau| samādhiparibhāvitā pañ‍ñā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| pañ‍ñāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ sammadaiva āsavaihi vimuc‍cati, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmāsavā, bhavāsavā, avij‍jāsavā’’ti|

kammāraputtacundavatthu

189. atha khau bhagavā bhauganagarai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina pāvā tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina pāvā tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā pāvāyaṃ viharati cundassa kammāraputtassa ambavanai| assausi khau cundau kammāraputtau – ‘‘bhagavā kira pāvaṃ anuppattau, pāvāyaṃ viharati mayhaṃ ambavanai’’ti| atha khau cundau kammāraputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau cundaṃ kammāraputtaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau cundau kammāraputtau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau cundau kammāraputtau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ  viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau cundau kammāraputtau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā pahūtañ‍ca sūkaramaddavaṃ bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina cundassa kammāraputtassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā cundaṃ kammāraputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yaṃ tai, cunda, sūkaramaddavaṃ paṭiyattaṃ, taina maṃ parivisa| yaṃ panañ‍ñaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyattaṃ, taina bhikkhusaṅghaṃ  parivisā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau cundau kammāraputtau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaṃ ahausi sūkaramaddavaṃ paṭiyattaṃ, taina bhagavantaṃ parivisi| yaṃ panañ‍ñaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyattaṃ, taina bhikkhusaṅghaṃ parivisi| atha khau bhagavā cundaṃ kammāraputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yaṃ tai, cunda, sūkaramaddavaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ, taṃ saubbhai nikhaṇāhi| nāhaṃ taṃ, cunda, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yassa taṃ paribhuttaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchaiyya añ‍ñatra tathāgatassā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau cundau kammāraputtau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaṃ ahausi sūkaramaddavaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ, taṃ saubbhai nikhaṇitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ  khau cundaṃ kammāraputtaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

190. atha khau bhagavatau cundassa kammāraputtassa bhattaṃ bhuttāvissa kharau ābādhau uppaj‍ji, lauhitapakkhandikā pabāḷhā vaidanā vattanti māraṇantikā| tā sudaṃ  bhagavā satau sampajānau adhivāsaisi avihañ‍ñamānau| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina kusinārā tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

cundassa bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā, kammārassāti mai sutaṃ|

ābādhaṃ samphusī dhīrau, pabāḷhaṃ māraṇantikaṃ||

bhuttassa ca sūkaramaddavaina,

byādhippabāḷhau udapādi satthunau|

viraicamānau viric‍camānau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), viriñ‍camānau (?) bhagavā avauca,

gacchāmahaṃ kusināraṃ nagaranti||

pānīyāharaṇaṃ

191. atha khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma yaina añ‍ñataraṃ rukkhamūlaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, ānanda, catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñapaihi, kilantausmi, ānanda, nisīdissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau  bhagavatau paṭissutvā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñapaisi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ  ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, ānanda, pānīyaṃ āhara, pipāsitausmi, ānanda, pivissāmī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idāni, bhantai, pañ‍camattāni sakaṭasatāni atik‍kantāni, taṃ cak‍kacchin‍naṃ udakaṃ parittaṃ luḷitaṃ āvilaṃ sandati| ayaṃ, bhantai, kakudhā kakuthā (sī॰ pī॰) nadī avidūrai acchaudakā sātaudakā  sītaudakā saitaudakā saitakā (sī॰) suppatitthā ramaṇīyā| aittha bhagavā pānīyañ‍ca pivissati, gattāni ca sītī sītaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) karissatī’’ti|

dutiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, ānanda, pānīyaṃ āhara, pipāsitausmi, ānanda, pivissāmī’’ti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idāni, bhantai, pañ‍camattāni sakaṭasatāni atik‍kantāni, taṃ cak‍kacchin‍naṃ udakaṃ parittaṃ luḷitaṃ āvilaṃ sandati| ayaṃ, bhantai, kakudhā nadī avidūrai acchaudakā sātaudakā sītaudakā saitaudakā suppatitthā ramaṇīyā| aittha bhagavā pānīyañ‍ca pivissati, gattāni ca sītīkarissatī’’ti|

tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, ānanda, pānīyaṃ āhara, pipāsitausmi, ānanda, pivissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā  pattaṃ gahaitvā yaina sā nadikā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sā nadikā cak‍kacchin‍nā parittā luḷitā āvilā sandamānā, āyasmantai ānandai upasaṅkamantai acchā vippasan‍nā anāvilā sandittha sandati (syā॰)| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau, tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā| ayañhi sā nadikā cak‍kacchin‍nā parittā luḷitā āvilā sandamānā mayi upasaṅkamantai acchā vippasan‍nā anāvilā sandatī’’ti| pattaina pānīyaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai, tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā| idāni sā bhantai nadikā cak‍kacchin‍nā parittā luḷitā āvilā sandamānā mayi upasaṅkamantai acchā vippasan‍nā anāvilā sandittha| pivatu bhagavā pānīyaṃ pivatu sugatau pānīya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pānīyaṃ apāyi|

puk‍kusamal‍laputtavatthu

192. taina  khau pana samayaina puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau āḷārassa kālāmassa sāvakau kusinārāya pāvaṃ addhānamaggappaṭippan‍nau hauti| addasā khau puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ| disvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai, santaina vata, bhantai, pabbajitā vihāraina viharanti| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhantai, āḷārau kālāmau addhānamaggappaṭippan‍nau maggā auk‍kamma avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau, bhantai, pañ‍camattāni sakaṭasatāni āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ nissāya nissāya atik‍kamiṃsu| atha khau, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau purisau tassa sakaṭasatthassa sakaṭasatassa (ka॰) piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau āgacchantau yaina āḷārau kālāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ aitadavauca – ‘api, bhantai, pañ‍camattāni sakaṭasatāni atik‍kantāni addasā’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, addasa’nti| ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, saddaṃ assausī’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, saddaṃ assausi’nti| ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, suttau ahausī’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, suttau ahausi’nti| ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, sañ‍ñī ahausī’ti? ‘aivamāvusau’ti| ‘sau tvaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñī samānau jāgarau pañ‍camattāni sakaṭasatāni nissāya nissāya atik‍kantāni naiva addasa, na pana saddaṃ assausi; apisu api hi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tai, bhantai, saṅghāṭi rajaina aukiṇṇā’ti? ‘aivamāvusau’ti| atha khau, bhantai, tassa purisassa aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, santaina vata bhau pabbajitā vihāraina viharanti| yatra hi nāma sañ‍ñī samānau  jāgarau pañ‍camattāni sakaṭasatāni nissāya nissāya atik‍kantāni naiva dakkhati, na pana saddaṃ saussatī’ti! āḷārai kālāmai uḷāraṃ pasādaṃ pavaidaitvā pak‍kāmī’’ti|

193. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, puk‍kusa, katamaṃ nu khau duk‍karataraṃ vā durabhisambhavataraṃ vā – yau vā sañ‍ñī samānau jāgarau pañ‍camattāni  sakaṭasatāni nissāya nissāya atik‍kantāni naiva passaiyya, na pana saddaṃ  suṇaiyya; yau vā sañ‍ñī samānau jāgarau daivai vassantai daivai gaḷagaḷāyantai vij‍jul‍latāsu vij‍jutāsu (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) niccharantīsu asaniyā phalantiyā naiva passaiyya, na pana saddaṃ suṇaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi, bhantai, karissanti pañ‍ca vā sakaṭasatāni cha vā sakaṭasatāni satta vā sakaṭasatāni aṭṭha vā sakaṭasatāni nava vā sakaṭasatāni nava vā sakaṭasatāni dasa vā sakaṭasatāni (sī॰), sakaṭasahassaṃ vā sakaṭasatasahassaṃ vā| atha khau aitadaiva duk‍karataraṃ caiva durabhisambhavatarañ‍ca yau  sañ‍ñī samānau jāgarau daivai vassantai daivai gaḷagaḷāyantai vij‍jul‍latāsu niccharantīsu asaniyā phalantiyā naiva passaiyya, na pana saddaṃ suṇaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, puk‍kusa, samayaṃ ātumāyaṃ viharāmi bhusāgārai| taina khau pana samayaina daivai vassantai daivai gaḷagaḷāyantai vij‍jul‍latāsu niccharantīsu asaniyā phalantiyā avidūrai bhusāgārassa dvai kassakā bhātarau hatā cattārau ca balibaddā balibaddā (sī॰ pī॰)| atha khau, puk‍kusa, ātumāya mahājanakāyau nikkhamitvā yaina tai dvai kassakā bhātarau hatā cattārau ca balibaddā tainupasaṅkami| taina khau panāhaṃ, puk‍kusa, samayaina bhusāgārā nikkhamitvā bhusāgāradvārai abbhaukāsai caṅkamāmi| atha khau, puk‍kusa, añ‍ñatarau purisau tamhā mahājanakāyā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitaṃ khau ahaṃ, puk‍kusa, taṃ purisaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khau aisau, āvusau, mahājanakāyau san‍nipatitau’ti? ‘idāni, bhantai, daivai vassantai daivai gaḷagaḷāyantai vij‍jul‍latāsu niccharantīsu asaniyā phalantiyā dvai kassakā bhātarau hatā cattārau ca balibaddā| aitthaisau mahājanakāyau san‍nipatitau| tvaṃ pana, bhantai, k‍va ahausī’ti? ‘idhaiva khau ahaṃ, āvusau, ahausi’nti| ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, addasā’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, addasa’nti| ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, saddaṃ assausī’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, saddaṃ assausi’nti| ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, suttau ahausī’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, suttau ahausi’nti| ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, sañ‍ñī ahausī’ti? ‘aivamāvusau’ti| ‘sau tvaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñī samānau jāgarau daivai vassantai daivai gaḷagaḷāyantai  vij‍jul‍latāsu niccharantīsu asaniyā phalantiyā naiva addasa, na pana saddaṃ assausī’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti?

‘‘atha khau, puk‍kusa, purisassa aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, santaina vata bhau pabbajitā vihāraina viharanti| yatra hi nāma sañ‍ñī samānau jāgarau daivai vassantai daivai gaḷagaḷāyantai vij‍jul‍latāsu niccharantīsu asaniyā phalantiyā naiva dakkhati, na pana saddaṃ saussatī’ti suṇissati (syā॰)| mayi uḷāraṃ pasādaṃ pavaidaitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisāhaṃ, bhantai, yau mai āḷārai kālāmai pasādau taṃ mahāvātai vā auphuṇāmi sīghasautāya  siṅghasautāya (ka॰) vā nadiyā pavāhaimi| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ  vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca  bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

194. atha khau puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, bhaṇai, siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ yugamaṭṭhaṃ dhāraṇīyaṃ āharā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau purisau puk‍kusassa mal‍laputtassa paṭissutvā taṃ siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ yugamaṭṭhaṃ dhāraṇīyaṃ āhari āharasi (ka॰)| atha khau puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau taṃ siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ yugamaṭṭhaṃ dhāraṇīyaṃ bhagavatau upanāmaisi – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ yugamaṭṭhaṃ dhāraṇīyaṃ, taṃ mai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, puk‍kusa, aikaina maṃ acchādaihi, aikaina ānanda’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau bhagavatau paṭissutvā aikaina bhagavantaṃ acchādaiti, aikaina āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ| atha khau bhagavā puk‍kusaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau puk‍kusau mal‍laputtau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

195. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau acirapak‍kantai puk‍kusai mal‍laputtai taṃ siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ yugamaṭṭhaṃ dhāraṇīyaṃ bhagavatau kāyaṃ upanāmaisi| taṃ bhagavatau kāyaṃ upanāmitaṃ hatac‍cikaṃ viya vītac‍cikaṃviya (sī॰ pī॰) khāyati| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai, yāva parisuddhau, bhantai, tathāgatassa chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| idaṃ, bhantai, siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ yugamaṭṭhaṃ dhāraṇīyaṃ bhagavatau  kāyaṃ upanāmitaṃ hatac‍cikaṃ viya khāyatī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, ānanda, aivamaitaṃ, ānanda dvīsu kālaisu ativiya tathāgatassa kāyau parisuddhau hauti chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| katamaisu dvīsu? yañ‍ca, ānanda, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati| imaisu khau, ānanda, dvīsu kālaisu ativiya tathāgatassa kāyau parisuddhau hauti chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| ‘‘aj‍ja khau, panānanda, rattiyā pacchimai yāmai kusinārāyaṃ upavattanai mal‍lānaṃ sālavanai antaraina antarai (syā॰) yamakasālānaṃ tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati bhavissatīti (ka॰)| āyāmānanda, yaina kakudhā nadī tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ  yugamaṭṭhaṃ, puk‍kusau abhihārayi|

taina acchāditau satthā, haimavaṇṇau asaubhathāti||

196. atha khau bhagavā mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina kakudhā nadī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā kakudhaṃ nadiṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā nhatvā ca pivitvā ca pac‍cuttaritvā yaina ambavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ cundakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, cundaka, catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñapaihi, kilantausmi, cundaka, nipaj‍jissāmī’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā cundakau bhagavatau paṭissutvā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñapaisi| atha khau bhagavā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaisi pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ  manasikaritvā| āyasmā pana cundakau tatthaiva bhagavatau puratau nisīdi|

gantvāna  buddhau nadikaṃ kakudhaṃ,

acchaudakaṃ sātudakaṃ vippasan‍naṃ|

augāhi satthā akilantarūpau sukilantarūpau (sī॰ pī॰),

tathāgatau appaṭimau ca appaṭimaudha (pī॰) laukai||

nhatvā ca pivitvā cudatāri satthā pivitvā cundakaina, pivitvā ca uttari (ka॰),

purakkhatau bhikkhugaṇassa majjhai|

vattā satthā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pavattā bhagavā idha dhammai,

upāgami ambavanaṃ mahaisi||

āmantayi cundakaṃ nāma bhikkhuṃ,

catugguṇaṃ  santhara mai nipaj‍jaṃ|

sau cauditau bhāvitattaina cundau,

catugguṇaṃ santhari khippamaiva||

nipaj‍ji satthā akilantarūpau,

cundaupi tattha pamukhai samukhai (ka॰) nisīdīti||

197. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘siyā khau yau khau (ka॰), panānanda, cundassa  kammāraputtassa kauci vippaṭisāraṃ uppādaiyya – ‘tassa tai, āvusau cunda, alābhā tassa tai dul‍laddhaṃ, yassa tai tathāgatau pacchimaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā parinibbutau’ti| cundassa, ānanda, kammāraputtassa aivaṃ vippaṭisārau paṭivinaitabbau – ‘tassa tai, āvusau cunda, lābhā tassa tai suladdhaṃ, yassa tai tathāgatau pacchimaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā parinibbutau| sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau cunda, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – dvai mai piṇḍapātā samasamaphalā  samā samaphalā (ka॰) samavipākā samasamavipākā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), ativiya añ‍ñaihi piṇḍapātaihi mahapphalatarā ca mahānisaṃsatarā ca| katamai dvai? yañ‍ca piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, yañ‍ca piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā tathāgatau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati| imai dvai piṇḍapātā samasamaphalā samavipākā, ativiya añ‍ñaihi piṇḍapātaihi mahapphalatarā ca mahānisaṃsatarā ca| āyusaṃvattanikaṃ āyasmatā cundaina kammāraputtaina kammaṃ upacitaṃ, vaṇṇasaṃvattanikaṃ āyasmatā cundaina kammāraputtaina kammaṃ upacitaṃ, sukhasaṃvattanikaṃ āyasmatā cundaina kammāraputtaina kammaṃ upacitaṃ, yasasaṃvattanikaṃ āyasmatā cundaina kammāraputtaina kammaṃ upacitaṃ, saggasaṃvattanikaṃ  āyasmatā cundaina kammāraputtaina kammaṃ upacitaṃ, ādhipataiyyasaṃvattanikaṃ āyasmatā cundaina kammāraputtaina kammaṃ upacita’nti| cundassa, ānanda, kammāraputtassa aivaṃ vippaṭisārau paṭivinaitabbau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati,

saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyati|

kusalau ca jahāti pāpakaṃ,

rāgadausamauhakkhayā sanibbutau’’ti||

catutthau bhāṇavārau|

yamakasālā

198. atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina hirañ‍ñavatiyā nadiyā pārimaṃ tīraṃ, yaina kusinārā upavattanaṃ mal‍lānaṃ sālavanaṃ tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina hirañ‍ñavatiyā nadiyā pārimaṃ tīraṃ, yaina kusinārā upavattanaṃ mal‍lānaṃ sālavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, ānanda, antaraina yamakasālānaṃ uttarasīsakaṃ mañ‍cakaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi, kilantausmi, ānanda, nipaj‍jissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā antaraina yamakasālānaṃ uttarasīsakaṃ mañ‍cakaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi| atha khau bhagavā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaisi pādai  pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau|

taina  khau pana samayaina yamakasālā sabbaphāliphul‍lā haunti akālapupphaihi| tai tathāgatassa sarīraṃ aukiranti ajjhaukiranti abhippakiranti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi mandāravapupphāni antalikkhā papatanti, tāni tathāgatassa sarīraṃ aukiranti ajjhaukiranti abhippakiranti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi candanacuṇṇāni antalikkhā papatanti, tāni tathāgatassa sarīraṃ aukiranti  ajjhaukiranti abhippakiranti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi tūriyāni antalikkhai vaj‍janti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi saṅgītāni antalikkhai vattanti tathāgatassa pūjāya|

199. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sabbaphāliphul‍lā khau, ānanda, yamakasālā akālapupphaihi| tai tathāgatassa sarīraṃ aukiranti ajjhaukiranti abhippakiranti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi mandāravapupphāni antalikkhā papatanti, tāni tathāgatassa sarīraṃ aukiranti ajjhaukiranti abhippakiranti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi candanacuṇṇāni antalikkhā papatanti, tāni tathāgatassa sarīraṃ aukiranti ajjhaukiranti abhippakiranti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi tūriyāni antalikkhai vaj‍janti tathāgatassa pūjāya| dibbānipi saṅgītāni antalikkhai vattanti tathāgatassa pūjāya| na khau, ānanda, aittāvatā tathāgatau sak‍katau vā hauti garukatau vā mānitau vā pūjitau vā apacitau vā| yau khau, ānanda, bhikkhu  vā bhikkhunī vā upāsakau vā upāsikā vā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharati sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, sau tathāgataṃ sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti apaciyati idaṃ padaṃ sīsyāipautthakaisu na dissati, paramāya pūjāya| tasmātihānanda, dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā viharissāma sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinauti| aivañhi vau, ānanda, sikkhitabba’’nti|

upavāṇatthairau

200. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā upavāṇau bhagavatau puratau ṭhitau hauti bhagavantaṃ bījayamānau| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ apasāraisi – ‘‘apaihi, bhikkhu, mā mai puratau aṭṭhāsī’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau  āyasmā  upavāṇau dīgharattaṃ bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau santikāvacarau samīpacārī| atha ca pana bhagavā pacchimai kālai āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ apasāraiti – ‘apaihi bhikkhu, mā mai puratau aṭṭhāsī’ti| kau nu khau haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaṃ bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ apasāraiti – ‘apaihi, bhikkhu, mā mai puratau aṭṭhāsī’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘ayaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā upavāṇau dīgharattaṃ bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau santikāvacarau samīpacārī| atha ca pana bhagavā pacchimai kālai āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ apasāraiti – ‘‘apaihi, bhikkhu, mā mai puratau aṭṭhāsī’’ti| kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaṃ bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ apasāraiti – ‘‘apaihi, bhikkhu, mā mai puratau aṭṭhāsī’’ti? ‘‘yaibhuyyaina, ānanda, dasasu laukadhātūsu daivatā san‍nipatitā  tathāgataṃ dassanāya| yāvatā, ānanda, kusinārā upavattanaṃ mal‍lānaṃ sālavanaṃ samantatau dvādasa yaujanāni, natthi sau padaisau vālaggakauṭinitudanamattaupi mahaisakkhāhi daivatāhi apphuṭau| daivatā, ānanda, ujjhāyanti – ‘dūrā ca vatamha āgatā tathāgataṃ dassanāya| kadāci karahaci tathāgatā laukai uppaj‍janti arahantau sammāsambuddhā| aj‍jaiva rattiyā pacchimai yāmai tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| ayañ‍ca mahaisakkhau bhikkhu bhagavatau puratau ṭhitau auvāraintau, na mayaṃ labhāma pacchimai kālai tathāgataṃ dassanāyā’’’ti|

201. ‘‘kathaṃbhūtā pana, bhantai, bhagavā daivatā manasikarautī’’ti manasi karauntīti (syā॰ ka॰)? ‘‘santānanda, daivatā ākāsai pathavīsañ‍ñiniyau kaisai pakiriya kandanti, bāhā paggayha kandanti, chin‍napātaṃ papatanti chin‍naṃpādaṃviya papatanti (syā॰), āvaṭṭanti, vivaṭṭanti – ‘atikhippaṃ bhagavā parinibbāyissati, atikhippaṃ sugatau parinibbāyissati, atikhippaṃ cakkhuṃ cakkhumā (syā॰ ka॰) laukai antaradhaṃāyissatī’ti|

‘‘santānanda, daivatā pathaviyaṃ pathavīsañ‍ñiniyau kaisai pakiriya kandanti, bāhā paggayha kandanti, chin‍napātaṃ  papatanti, āvaṭṭanti, vivaṭṭanti – ‘atikhippaṃ bhagavā parinibbāyissati, atikhippaṃ sugatau parinibbāyissati, atikhippaṃ cakkhuṃ laukai antaradhāyissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yā  pana tā daivatā vītarāgā, tā satā sampajānā adhivāsainti – ‘anic‍cā saṅkhārā, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti|

catusaṃvaijanīyaṭṭhānāni

202. ‘‘pubbai, bhantai, disāsu vassaṃ vuṭṭhā vassaṃvutthā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhikkhū āgacchanti tathāgataṃ dassanāya| tai mayaṃ labhāma manaubhāvanīyai bhikkhū dassanāya, labhāma payirupāsanāya| bhagavatau pana mayaṃ, bhantai, ac‍cayaina na labhissāma manaubhāvanīyai bhikkhū dassanāya, na labhissāma payirupāsanāyā’’ti|

‘‘cattārimāni, ānanda, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyāni saṃvaijanīyāni ṭhānāni| katamāni cattāri? ‘idha tathāgatau jātau’ti, ānanda, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| ‘idha tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti, ānanda, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| ‘idha tathāgataina anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattita’nti, ānanda, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| ‘idha tathāgatau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbutau’ti, ānanda, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| imāni khau, ānanda, cattāri saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyāni saṃvaijanīyāni ṭhānāni|

‘‘āgamissanti khau, ānanda, saddhā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau – ‘idha tathāgatau jātau’tipi, ‘idha tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’tipi, ‘idha tathāgataina anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattita’ntipi, ‘idha tathāgatau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbutau’tipi| yai hi kaici, ānanda, caitiyacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā pasan‍nacittā kālaṅkarissanti, sabbai tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissantī’’ti|

ānandapucchākathā

203. ‘‘kathaṃ  mayaṃ, bhantai, mātugāmai paṭipaj‍jāmā’’ti? ‘‘adassanaṃ, ānandā’’ti| ‘‘dassanai, bhagavā, sati kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? ‘‘anālāpau, ānandā’’ti| ‘‘ālapantaina pana, bhantai, kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? ‘‘sati, ānanda, upaṭṭhāpaitabbā’’ti|

204. ‘‘kathaṃ  mayaṃ, bhantai, tathāgatassa sarīrai paṭipaj‍jāmā’’ti? ‘‘abyāvaṭā tumhai, ānanda, hautha tathāgatassa sarīrapūjāya| iṅgha tumhai, ānanda, sāratthai ghaṭatha anuyuñ‍jatha sadatthai anuyuñ‍jatha (sī॰ syā॰), sadatthaṃ anuyuñ‍jatha (pī॰), sāratthai anuyuñ‍jatha (ka॰), sāratthai appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharatha| santānanda, khattiyapaṇḍitāpi brāhmaṇapaṇḍitāpi gahapatipaṇḍitāpi tathāgatai abhippasan‍nā, tai tathāgatassa sarīrapūjaṃ karissantī’’ti|

205. ‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, tathāgatassa sarīrai paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? ‘‘yathā khau, ānanda, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa sarīrai paṭipaj‍janti, aivaṃ tathāgatassa sarīrai paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti| ‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa sarīrai paṭipaj‍jantī’’ti? ‘‘rañ‍ñau, ānanda, cak‍kavattissa sarīraṃ ahataina vatthaina vaiṭhainti, ahataina vatthaina vaiṭhaitvā vihataina kappāsaina vaiṭhainti, vihataina kappāsaina vaiṭhaitvā ahataina vatthaina  vaiṭhainti| aitainupāyaina pañ‍cahi yugasataihi rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa sarīraṃ sarīrai (syā॰ ka॰) vaiṭhaitvā āyasāya tailadauṇiyā pakkhipitvā añ‍ñissā āyasāya dauṇiyā paṭikuj‍jitvā sabbagandhānaṃ citakaṃ karitvā rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa sarīraṃ jhāpainti| cātumahāpathai cātummahāpathai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa thūpaṃ karaunti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa sarīrai paṭipaj‍janti| yathā khau, ānanda, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa sarīrai paṭipaj‍janti, aivaṃ tathāgatassa sarīrai paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ| cātumahāpathai tathāgatassa thūpau kātabbau| tattha yai mālaṃ vā gandhaṃ vā cuṇṇakaṃ vaṇṇakaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vā āraupaissanti vā abhivādaissanti vā cittaṃ vā pasādaissanti taisaṃ taṃ bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya|

thūpārahapuggalau

206. ‘‘cattāraumai, ānanda, thūpārahā| katamai cattārau? tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau thūpārahau, pac‍caikasambuddhau thūpārahau, tathāgatassa sāvakau thūpārahau, rājā cak‍kavattī cak‍kavatti (syā॰ ka॰) thūpārahauti|

‘‘kiñ‍cānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau thūpārahau? ‘ayaṃ tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa thūpau’ti, ānanda, bahujanā cittaṃ pasādainti| tai tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| idaṃ khau, ānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau thūpārahau|

‘‘kiñ‍cānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍caikasambuddhau thūpārahau? ‘ayaṃ tassa bhagavatau pac‍caikasambuddhassa  thūpau’ti, ānanda, bahujanā cittaṃ pasādainti| tai tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| idaṃ khau, ānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍caikasambuddhau thūpārahau|

‘‘kiñ‍cānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca tathāgatassa sāvakau  thūpārahau? ‘ayaṃ tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa sāvakassa thūpau’ti ānanda, bahujanā cittaṃ pasādainti| tai tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| idaṃ khau, ānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca tathāgatassa sāvakau thūpārahau|

‘‘kiñ‍cānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca rājā cak‍kavattī thūpārahau? ‘ayaṃ tassa dhammikassa dhammarañ‍ñau thūpau’ti, ānanda, bahujanā cittaṃ pasādainti| tai tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| idaṃ khau, ānanda, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca rājā cak‍kavattī thūpārahau| imai khau, ānanda cattārau thūpārahā’’ti|

ānandaacchariyadhammau

207. atha khau āyasmā ānandau vihāraṃ pavisitvā kapisīsaṃ ālambitvā raudamānau aṭṭhāsi – ‘‘ahañ‍ca vatamhi saikhau sakaraṇīyau, satthu ca mai parinibbānaṃ bhavissati, yau mama anukampakau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, ānandau’’ti? ‘‘aisau, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau vihāraṃ pavisitvā kapisīsaṃ ālambitvā raudamānau ṭhitau – ‘ahañ‍ca vatamhi saikhau sakaraṇīyau, satthu ca mai parinibbānaṃ bhavissati, yau mama anukampakau’’’ti| atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina ānandaṃ āmantaihi  – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau ānanda, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau ānanda, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, ānanda, mā sauci mā paridaivi, nanu aitaṃ, ānanda, mayā paṭikac‍caiva akkhātaṃ – ‘sabbaihaiva piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau añ‍ñathābhāvau’; taṃ kutaittha, ānanda, labbhā| yaṃ taṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ  palaukadhammaṃ, taṃ vata tathāgatassāpi sarīraṃ mā paluj‍jī’ti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| dīgharattaṃ khau tai, ānanda, tathāgatau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau maittaina kāyakammaina hitaina sukhaina advayaina appamāṇaina, maittaina vacīkammaina hitaina sukhaina advayaina appamāṇaina, maittaina manaukammaina hitaina sukhaina advayaina appamāṇaina| katapuñ‍ñausi tvaṃ, ānanda, padhānamanuyuñ‍ja, khippaṃ hauhisi anāsavau’’ti|

208. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, taisampi bhagavantānaṃ aitapparamāyaiva upaṭṭhākā ahaisuṃ, saiyyathāpi mayhaṃ ānandau| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, taisampi bhagavantānaṃ aitapparamāyaiva upaṭṭhākā bhavissanti, saiyyathāpi mayhaṃ ānandau| paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, ānandau; maidhāvī, bhikkhavai, ānandau| jānāti ‘ayaṃ kālau tathāgataṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ayaṃ kālau bhikkhunīnaṃ, ayaṃ kālau upāsakānaṃ, ayaṃ  kālau upāsikānaṃ, ayaṃ kālau rañ‍ñau rājamahāmattānaṃ titthiyānaṃ titthiyasāvakāna’nti|

209. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, acchariyā abbhutā dhammā abbhutadhammā (syā॰ ka॰) ānandai| katamai cattārau? sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ  bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā hauti, atha khau ānandau tuṇhī hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīparisā hauti, atha khau ānandau tuṇhī hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, upāsakaparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, upāsakaparisā hauti, atha khau ānandau tuṇhī hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, upāsikāparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai, ānandau, dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, upāsikāparisā hauti, atha khau ānandau tuṇhī hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, acchariyā abbhutā dhammā rañ‍ñai cak‍kavattimhi| katamai cattārau? sacai, bhikkhavai, khattiyaparisā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ dassanāya  upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai rājā cak‍kavattī bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, khattiyaparisā hauti| atha khau rājā cak‍kavattī tuṇhī hauti| sacai bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇaparisā…pai॰… gahapatiparisā…pai॰… samaṇaparisā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai rājā cak‍kavattī bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, samaṇaparisā hauti, atha khau rājā cak‍kavattī tuṇhī hauti| aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, cattāraumai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā hauti| atha khau ānandau tuṇhī hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai bhikkhunīparisā…pai॰… upāsakaparisā…pai॰… upāsikāparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanaina sā attamanā  hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, upāsikāparisā hauti| atha khau ānandau tuṇhī hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai’’ti|

mahāsudassanasuttadaisanā

210. aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, bhantai, bhagavā imasmiṃ khuddakanagarakai uj‍jaṅgalanagarakai sākhānagarakai parinibbāyi| santi, bhantai, añ‍ñāni mahānagarāni, saiyyathidaṃ – campā  rājagahaṃ sāvatthī sākaitaṃ kausambī bārāṇasī; aittha bhagavā parinibbāyatu| aittha bahū khattiyamahāsālā, brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatimahāsālā tathāgatai abhippasan‍nā| tai tathāgatassa sarīrapūjaṃ karissantī’’ti ‘‘māhaivaṃ, ānanda, avaca; māhaivaṃ, ānanda, avaca – ‘khuddakanagarakaṃ uj‍jaṅgalanagarakaṃ sākhānagaraka’nti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, ānanda, rājā mahāsudassanau nāma ahausi cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janappadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| rañ‍ñau, ānanda, mahāsudassanassa ayaṃ kusinārā kusāvatī nāma rājadhānī ahausi, puratthimaina ca pacchimaina ca dvādasayaujanāni āyāmaina; uttaraina ca dakkhiṇaina ca sattayaujanāni vitthāraina| kusāvatī, ānanda, rājadhānī iddhā caiva ahausi phītā ca  bahujanā ca ākiṇṇamanussā ca subhikkhā ca| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, daivānaṃ āḷakamandā nāma rājadhānī iddhā caiva hauti phītā  ca bahujanā ca ākiṇṇayakkhā ca subhikkhā ca; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, kusāvatī rājadhānī iddhā caiva ahausi phītā ca bahujanā ca ākiṇṇamanussā ca subhikkhā ca| kusāvatī, ānanda, rājadhānī dasahi saddaihi avivittā ahausi divā caiva rattiñ‍ca, saiyyathidaṃ – hatthisaddaina assasaddaina rathasaddaina bhairisaddaina mudiṅgasaddaina vīṇāsaddaina gītasaddaina saṅkhasaddaina sammasaddaina pāṇitāḷasaddaina ‘asnātha pivatha khādathā’ti dasamaina saddaina|

‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, ānanda, kusināraṃ pavisitvā kausinārakānaṃ mal‍lānaṃ  āraucaihi – ‘aj‍ja khau, vāsaiṭṭhā, rattiyā pacchimai yāmai tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ  bhavissati| abhik‍kamatha vāsaiṭṭhā, abhik‍kamatha vāsaiṭṭhā| mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha – amhākañ‍ca nau gāmakkhaittai tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ ahausi, na mayaṃ labhimhā pacchimai kālai tathāgataṃ dassanāyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya attadutiyau kusināraṃ pāvisi|

mal‍lānaṃ vandanā

211. taina khau pana samayaina kausinārakā mal‍lā sandhāgārai santhāgārai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) san‍nipatitā haunti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina kausinārakānaṃ mal‍lānaṃ sandhāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā kausinārakānaṃ mal‍lānaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘aj‍ja khau, vāsaiṭṭhā, rattiyā pacchimai yāmai tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| abhik‍kamatha vāsaiṭṭhā abhik‍kamatha vāsaiṭṭhā| mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha – ‘amhākañ‍ca nau gāmakkhaittai tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ  ahausi, na mayaṃ labhimhā pacchimai kālai tathāgataṃ dassanāyā’’’ti| idamāyasmatau ānandassa vacanaṃ sutvā mal‍lā ca mal‍laputtā ca mal‍lasuṇisā ca mal‍lapajāpatiyau ca aghāvinau dummanā caitaudukkhasamappitā appaikac‍cai kaisai pakiriya kandanti, bāhā paggayha kandanti, chin‍napātaṃ papatanti, āvaṭṭanti vivaṭṭanti – ‘atikhippaṃ bhagavā parinibbāyissati, atikhippaṃ sugatau parinibbāyissati, atikhippaṃ cakkhuṃ  laukai antaradhāyissatī’ti| atha khau mal‍lā ca mal‍laputtā ca mal‍lasuṇisā ca mal‍lapajāpatiyau ca aghāvinau dummanā caitaudukkhasamappitā yaina upavattanaṃ mal‍lānaṃ sālavanaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau ahaṃ kausinārakai mal‍lai aikamaikaṃ bhagavantaṃ vandāpaissāmi, avanditau bhagavā kausinārakaihi mal‍laihi bhavissati, athāyaṃ ratti vibhāyissati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kausinārakai mal‍lai kulaparivattasau kulaparivattasau  ṭhapaitvā bhagavantaṃ vandāpaiyyaṃ – ‘itthan‍nāmau, bhantai, mal‍lau saputtau sabhariyau saparisau sāmac‍cau bhagavatau pādai  sirasā vandatī’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau kausinārakai mal‍lai kulaparivattasau kulaparivattasau ṭhapaitvā bhagavantaṃ vandāpaisi – ‘itthan‍nāmau, bhantai, mal‍lau saputtau sabhariyau saparisau sāmac‍cau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau aitaina upāyaina paṭhamainaiva yāmaina kausinārakai mal‍lai bhagavantaṃ vandāpaisi|

subhaddaparibbājakavatthu

212. taina khau pana samayaina subhaddau nāma paribbājakau kusinārāyaṃ paṭivasati| assausi khau subhaddau paribbājakau – ‘‘aj‍ja kira rattiyā pacchimai yāmai samaṇassa gautamassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissatī’’ti| atha  khau subhaddassa paribbājakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ paribbājakānaṃ vuḍḍhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘kadāci karahaci tathāgatā laukai uppaj‍janti arahantau sammāsambuddhā’ti| aj‍jaiva  rattiyā pacchimai yāmai samaṇassa gautamassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| atthi ca mai ayaṃ kaṅkhādhammau uppan‍nau, aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ samaṇai gautamai, ‘pahauti mai samaṇau gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ, yathāhaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyya’’’nti| atha khau subhaddau paribbājakau yaina upavattanaṃ mal‍lānaṃ sālavanaṃ, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau ānanda, paribbājakānaṃ vuḍḍhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘kadāci karahaci tathāgatā laukai uppaj‍janti arahantau  sammāsambuddhā’ti| aj‍jaiva rattiyā pacchimai yāmai samaṇassa gautamassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| atthi ca mai ayaṃ kaṅkhādhammau uppan‍nau – aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ samaṇai gautamai ‘pahauti mai samaṇau gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ, yathāhaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyya’nti| sādhāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, labhaiyyaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau subhaddaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau subhadda, mā tathāgataṃ vihaiṭhaisi, kilantau bhagavā’’ti| dutiyampi khau subhaddau paribbājakau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau subhaddau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau ānanda, paribbājakānaṃ vuḍḍhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘kadāci karahaci tathāgatā  laukai uppaj‍janti arahantau sammāsambuddhā’ti| aj‍jaiva rattiyā pacchimai yāmai samaṇassa  gautamassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| atthi ca mai ayaṃ kaṅkhādhammau uppan‍nau – aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ samaṇai gautamai, ‘pahauti mai samaṇau gautamau tathā  dhammaṃ daisaituṃ, yathāhaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyya’nti| sādhāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, labhaiyyaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau subhaddaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau subhadda, mā tathāgataṃ vihaiṭhaisi, kilantau bhagavā’’ti|

213. assausi khau bhagavā āyasmatau ānandassa subhaddaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘alaṃ, ānanda, mā subhaddaṃ vāraisi, labhataṃ, ānanda, subhaddau tathāgataṃ dassanāya| yaṃ kiñ‍ci maṃ subhaddau pucchissati, sabbaṃ taṃ añ‍ñāpaikkhauva pucchissati, nau vihaisāpaikkhau| yaṃ cassāhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarissāmi, taṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau subhaddaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gacchāvusau subhadda, karauti tai bhagavā aukāsa’’nti| atha khau subhaddau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau subhaddau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaimai, bhau gautama, samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṅghinau gaṇinau gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassinau titthakarā sādhusammatā bahujanassa, saiyyathidaṃ  – pūraṇau kassapau, makkhali gausālau, ajitau kaisakambalau, pakudhau kac‍cāyanau, sañ‍cayau bailaṭṭhaputtau, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau, sabbaitai sakāya paṭiñ‍ñāya abbhañ‍ñiṃsu, sabbaiva na abbhañ‍ñiṃsu, udāhu aikac‍cai abbhañ‍ñiṃsu, aikac‍cai na abbhañ‍ñiṃsū’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, subhadda, tiṭṭhataitaṃ – ‘sabbaitai sakāya paṭiñ‍ñāya abbhañ‍ñiṃsu, sabbaiva na abbhañ‍ñiṃsu, udāhu aikac‍cai abbhañ‍ñiṃsu, aikac‍cai na abbhañ‍ñiṃsū’ti| dhammaṃ tai, subhadda, daisaissāmi; taṃ suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau subhaddau paribbājakau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

214. ‘‘yasmiṃ khau, subhadda, dhammavinayai ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau na upalabbhati, samaṇaupi tattha na upalabbhati| dutiyaupi tattha samaṇau  na upalabbhati| tatiyaupi tattha samaṇau na upalabbhati| catutthaupi tattha samaṇau na upalabbhati| yasmiñ‍ca khau, subhadda, dhammavinayai ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau upalabbhati, samaṇaupi tattha upalabbhati, dutiyaupi tattha samaṇau upalabbhati, tatiyaupi tattha samaṇau upalabbhati, catutthaupi tattha samaṇau upalabbhati| imasmiṃ khau, subhadda, dhammavinayai ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau upalabbhati, idhaiva, subhadda, samaṇau, idha dutiyau samaṇau, idha tatiyau samaṇau, idha catutthau samaṇau, suñ‍ñā parappavādā samaṇaibhi  añ‍ñaihi añ‍ñai (pī॰)| imai  ca idhaiva (ka॰), subhadda, bhikkhū sammā viharaiyyuṃ, asuñ‍ñau laukau arahantaihi assāti|

‘‘aikūnatiṃsau vayasā subhadda,

yaṃ pabbajiṃ kiṃkusalānuaisī|

vassāni pañ‍ñāsa samādhikāni,

yatau ahaṃ pabbajitau subhadda||

ñāyassa dhammassa padaisavattī,

itau bahiddhā samaṇaupi natthi||

‘‘dutiyaupi  samaṇau natthi| tatiyaupi samaṇau natthi| catutthaupi samaṇau natthi| suñ‍ñā parappavādā samaṇaibhi añ‍ñaihi| imai ca, subhadda, bhikkhū sammā viharaiyyuṃ, asuñ‍ñau laukau arahantaihi assā’’ti|
